/**
 * Civil days in America/Sao_Paulo.
 *
 * Storage is unchanged: a civil day D is stored in a timestamptz as D T00:00:00Z
 * and read back with the UTC slice. Only decisions about "today" (the clock) go
 * through this module, because the UTC slice of an instant is one day ahead of
 * Sao Paulo between 21:00 and 23:59 local time.
 */
export const SAO_PAULO_TIME_ZONE = 'America/Sao_Paulo';

const ISO_DAY = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/;

const saoPauloDayFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {
  timeZone: SAO_PAULO_TIME_ZONE,
  year: 'numeric',
  month: '2-digit',
  day: '2-digit',
});

export function saoPauloDayOf(instant: Date): string {
  if (!(instant instanceof Date) || Number.isNaN(instant.getTime())) {
    throw new RangeError('invalid_instant');
  }
  const parts = saoPauloDayFormatter.formatToParts(instant);
  const part = (type: 'year' | 'month' | 'day') =>
    parts.find((candidate) => candidate.type === type)?.value ?? '';
  return `${part('year').padStart(4, '0')}-${part('month')}-${part('day')}`;
}

export function todayInSaoPaulo(now: Date = new Date()): string {
  return saoPauloDayOf(now);
}

export function isIsoDay(value: string): boolean {
  if (typeof value !== 'string') return false;
  const match = ISO_DAY.exec(value);
  if (!match) return false;
  const year = Number(match[1]);
  const month = Number(match[2]);
  const day = Number(match[3]);
  const probe = new Date(0);
  probe.setUTCFullYear(year, month - 1, day);
  return (
    probe.getUTCFullYear() === year &&
    probe.getUTCMonth() === month - 1 &&
    probe.getUTCDate() === day
  );
}

export function isAfterTodayInSaoPaulo(day: string, now: Date = new Date()): boolean {
  if (!isIsoDay(day)) throw new RangeError('invalid_iso_day');
  return day > todayInSaoPaulo(now);
}

/** Brazil has no daylight saving time since 2019, so a Sao Paulo midnight is 03:00Z. */
const SAO_PAULO_OFFSET_HOURS = 3;

/** The civil day after `day` (`YYYY-MM-DD`), by calendar arithmetic and never through the local timezone. */
export function addDaysToIsoDay(day: string, days: number): string {
  if (!isIsoDay(day)) throw new RangeError('invalid_iso_day');
  const [year, month, date] = day.split('-').map(Number) as [number, number, number];
  const moved = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, date + days));
  return moved.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

/**
 * A civil-day range in America/Sao_Paulo (both ends inclusive, both optional) as the half-open
 * instant interval `[start, endExclusive)`: `from` 00:00 -03:00 up to the day AFTER `to` 00:00 -03:00.
 * A lead created at 02:30Z on day D+1 is 23:30 on day D in Sao Paulo and belongs to day D.
 * Used for every period filter on a timestamp column; a `date` column compares the civil days directly.
 */
export function saoPauloDayRangeToInstants(
  from?: string | null,
  to?: string | null,
): { start: Date | null; endExclusive: Date | null } {
  const midnight = (day: string) => {
    const [year, month, date] = day.split('-').map(Number) as [number, number, number];
    return new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, date, SAO_PAULO_OFFSET_HOURS));
  };
  if (from != null && !isIsoDay(from)) throw new RangeError('invalid_iso_day');
  if (to != null && !isIsoDay(to)) throw new RangeError('invalid_iso_day');
  return {
    start: from == null ? null : midnight(from),
    endExclusive: to == null ? null : midnight(addDaysToIsoDay(to, 1)),
  };
}
import { isIsoDay, saoPauloDayRangeToInstants } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/sao-paulo-day';
import { eq, gte, lt, sql, type SQL } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { getDb } from '../../../db/client.js';
import { salesOpsCrmLeadFieldValues, salesOpsCrmLeads } from '../../../db/schema.js';
import { loadFieldDefinitions, type FieldDefinitionRow } from '../../crm/lead-fields.js';
import { defaultPipelineId } from '../../crm/pipeline-read.js';
import type { DynamicFilterInput } from './lead-schemas.js';

/**
 * The board read narrowing added by phase 2: pipeline, period (America/Sao_Paulo civil days over
 * `created_at`) and dynamic filters on FILTERABLE field definitions. One builder serves
 * `listLeads` and `summarizeLeadStages`, so a column's total and the summary's count always cover
 * the same set.
 */
type Db = ReturnType<typeof getDb>;

/** A filter key that is unknown, not filterable, or whose value does not fit its kind. The route answers 400. */
export class LeadFilterError extends Error {
  constructor(readonly filter: string) {
    super(`invalid_filter:${filter}`);
    this.name = 'LeadFilterError';
  }
}

const FILTER_KEY = /^f\.([a-z0-9_-]{1,60})(?:\.(min|max))?$/i;
const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

/** Reads every `f.<key>` / `f.<key>.min` / `f.<key>.max` entry of a query string. Any other `f.` shape is a 400. */
export function parseDynamicFilters(query: Record<string, string>): DynamicFilterInput[] {
  const filters: DynamicFilterInput[] = [];
  for (const [name, raw] of Object.entries(query)) {
    if (!name.startsWith('f.')) continue;
    const match = FILTER_KEY.exec(name);
    if (!match) throw new LeadFilterError(name);
    filters.push({ key: match[1]!, op: (match[2]?.toLowerCase() as 'min' | 'max' | undefined) ?? 'eq', raw });
  }
  return filters;
}

function valueExists(def: FieldDefinitionRow, predicate: SQL): SQL {
  return sql`EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM ${salesOpsCrmLeadFieldValues} v
    WHERE v.org_id = ${salesOpsCrmLeads.orgId} AND v.lead_id = ${salesOpsCrmLeads.id}
      AND v.field_id = ${def.id} AND ${predicate})`;
}

function escapeLike(raw: string): string {
  return raw.replace(/[\\%_]/g, (char) => `\\${char}`);
}

function condition(def: FieldDefinitionRow, filter: DynamicFilterInput): SQL {
  const bad = () => new LeadFilterError(`f.${filter.key}`);
  const range = filter.op !== 'eq';
  const raw = filter.raw.trim();
  if (raw === '') throw bad();
  if (def.backingColumn === 'client_id') {
    if (range || !UUID.test(raw)) throw bad();
    return eq(salesOpsCrmLeads.clientId, raw);
  }
  switch (def.kind) {
    case 'text':
      if (range) throw bad();
      return valueExists(def, sql`v.value_text ILIKE ${`%${escapeLike(raw)}%`} ESCAPE '\\'`);
    case 'options':
      if (range) throw bad();
      return valueExists(def, sql`v.value_option = ${raw}`);
    case 'number':
    case 'money': {
      const n = Number(raw);
      if (!Number.isFinite(n) || (def.kind === 'money' && !Number.isSafeInteger(n))) throw bad();
      const column = def.kind === 'number' ? sql`v.value_number` : sql`v.value_cents`;
      if (filter.op === 'min') return valueExists(def, sql`${column} >= ${n}`);
      if (filter.op === 'max') return valueExists(def, sql`${column} <= ${n}`);
      return valueExists(def, sql`${column} = ${n}`);
    }
    case 'date': {
      if (!isIsoDay(raw)) throw bad();
      if (filter.op === 'min') return valueExists(def, sql`v.value_date >= ${raw}::date`);
      if (filter.op === 'max') return valueExists(def, sql`v.value_date <= ${raw}::date`);
      return valueExists(def, sql`v.value_date = ${raw}::date`);
    }
    case 'reference': {
      if (range || !UUID.test(raw)) throw bad();
      switch (def.referenceTarget) {
        case 'cliente':
          return valueExists(def, sql`v.value_client_id = ${raw}::uuid`);
        case 'produto':
        case 'servico':
          return valueExists(def, sql`v.value_product_id = ${raw}::uuid`);
        case 'venda':
          return valueExists(def, sql`v.value_sale_id = ${raw}::uuid`);
        default:
          throw bad();
      }
    }
    default:
      throw bad();
  }
}

export type BoardNarrowing = {
  pipelineId?: string | undefined;
  from?: string | undefined;
  to?: string | undefined;
  filters?: DynamicFilterInput[] | undefined;
};

/**
 * The extra WHERE conjuncts of a board read. `defaultToDefaultPipeline` is true for the summary,
 * which has no stageId: it then covers the org's default pipeline unless one is named.
 */
export async function boardNarrowingConditions(
  tx: Db,
  orgId: string,
  narrowing: BoardNarrowing,
  options: { defaultToDefaultPipeline: boolean },
): Promise<SQL[]> {
  const conditions: SQL[] = [];
  const pipelineId =
    narrowing.pipelineId ?? (options.defaultToDefaultPipeline ? await defaultPipelineId(tx, orgId) : null);
  if (pipelineId) conditions.push(eq(salesOpsCrmLeads.pipelineId, pipelineId));

  const { start, endExclusive } = saoPauloDayRangeToInstants(narrowing.from, narrowing.to);
  if (start) conditions.push(gte(salesOpsCrmLeads.createdAt, start));
  if (endExclusive) conditions.push(lt(salesOpsCrmLeads.createdAt, endExclusive));

  if (narrowing.filters && narrowing.filters.length > 0) {
    const definitions = (await loadFieldDefinitions(tx, orgId)).filter((def) => def.status === 'active');
    for (const filter of narrowing.filters) {
      const def = definitions.find((candidate) => candidate.key === filter.key);
      if (!def || !def.filterable) throw new LeadFilterError(`f.${filter.key}`);
      conditions.push(condition(def, filter));
    }
  }
  return conditions;
}

import { isIsoDay } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/sao-paulo-day';
import { parseCurrencyInputToCents } from '../calculations';
import type { FieldDefinition, FieldKind, FieldReferenceItem, LeadFieldValue } from './crm-types';

import type { SalesOpsLead } from './types';

/**
 * Pure logic of the ONE LeadForm (both editions). No React, no fetch.
 * The write body is `LeadWritePayload` (api.ts), the single LeadWriteSchema shape.
 */

/** The Cliente system field key. It is carried ONLY by the top-level clientId/clientName. */
export const CLIENTE_FIELD_KEY = 'cliente';

/** The birth-date field key. It alone is capped at today (no future birthday). */
export const BIRTH_DATE_FIELD_KEY = 'nascimento';

export const LEAD_FORM_COPY = {
  createTitle: 'Novo lead',
  editTitle: 'Editar lead',

export type FieldDraft = string | FieldReferenceItem[];
export type LeadFormDraft = {
  contactName: string;
  contactPhone: string;
  contactEmail: string;
  clientId: string | null;
  clientName: string;
  estimatedValue: string;
  sellerPersonId: string | null;
  fields: Record<string, FieldDraft>;
};
/** `field` is `contactName`, `contact`, `contactEmail` or `field:<fieldId>`. */
export type LeadFormProblem = { field: string; message: string };

export function isClienteDefinition(definition: FieldDefinition): boolean {
  return definition.referenceTarget === 'cliente';
}

/** The dynamic fields the form renders: every definition except the Cliente, in position order. */
export function renderableDefinitions(definitions: readonly FieldDefinition[]): FieldDefinition[] {
  return definitions.filter((d) => !isClienteDefinition(d)).sort((a, b) => a.position - b.position);
}

const SEED_BY_KIND: Record<FieldKind, (value: LeadFieldValue['value'] | undefined) => FieldDraft> = {
  text: (v) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v : ''),
  number: (v) => (typeof v === 'number' ? String(v) : typeof v === 'string' ? v : ''),
  money: (v) => (typeof v === 'number' ? (v / 100).toFixed(2) : ''),
  date: (v) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v : ''),
  options: (v) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v : ''),
  reference: (v) => (Array.isArray(v) ? v.map((item) => ({ id: item.id, name: item.name })) : []),
};

export function leadFormDraftFromLead(lead: SalesOpsLead | null, definitions: readonly FieldDefinition[]): LeadFormDraft {
  const fields: Record<string, FieldDraft> = {};
  for (const definition of renderableDefinitions(definitions)) {
    const stored = lead?.fields?.find((f) => f.fieldId === definition.id)?.value;
    fields[definition.id] = SEED_BY_KIND[definition.kind](stored);
  }
  return {
    contactName: lead?.contactName ?? '',
    contactPhone: lead?.contactPhone ?? '',
    contactEmail: lead?.contactEmail ?? '',
    clientId: lead?.clientId ?? null,
    clientName: lead?.clientNameSnapshot ?? '',
    estimatedValue: lead?.estimatedValueBrl ? (lead.estimatedValueBrl / 100).toFixed(2) : '',
    sellerPersonId: lead?.sellerPersonId ?? null,
    fields,
  };
}

function hasStoredValue(value: LeadFieldValue['value'] | undefined): boolean {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') return false;
  return !(Array.isArray(value) && value.length === 0);
}

/** Ids of the dynamic fields that hold a value today; the Cliente is excluded (it is not a field entry). */
export function fieldIdsWithValue(lead: SalesOpsLead | null): Set<string> {
  const ids = new Set<string>();
  for (const field of lead?.fields ?? []) {
    if (field.key !== CLIENTE_FIELD_KEY && hasStoredValue(field.value)) ids.add(field.fieldId);
  }
  return ids;
}

export function splitClientContact(contact: string | null | undefined): { phone: string; email: string } {
  const trimmed = (contact ?? '').trim();
  if (trimmed === '') return { phone: '', email: '' };
  if (trimmed.includes('@')) return { phone: '', email: trimmed };
  return { phone: trimmed, email: '' };
}

/**
 * Prefills only the contact. A cliente has no responsible seller (no column), so the
 * vendedor is never touched. Typed text is never overwritten.
 */
export function prefillContactFromClient(
  draft: Pick<LeadFormDraft, 'contactPhone' | 'contactEmail'>,
  client: { contact: string | null } | undefined,
): Partial<Pick<LeadFormDraft, 'contactPhone' | 'contactEmail'>> {
  if (!client) return {};
  const split = splitClientContact(client.contact);
  const patch: Partial<Pick<LeadFormDraft, 'contactPhone' | 'contactEmail'>> = {};
  if (draft.contactPhone.trim() === '' && split.phone !== '') patch.contactPhone = split.phone;
  if (draft.contactEmail.trim() === '' && split.email !== '') patch.contactEmail = split.email;
  return patch;
}

const EMAIL_PATTERN = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

const FIELD_CHECK_BY_KIND: Record<FieldKind, (draft: FieldDraft | undefined) => string | null> = {
  number: (draft) => checkNumber(draft),
  money: (draft) => checkNumber(draft),
  date: (draft) => (typeof draft === 'string' && draft.trim() !== '' && !isIsoDay(draft.trim()) ? LEAD_FORM_COPY.dateInvalid : null),
  text: () => null,
  options: () => null,
  reference: () => null,
};

export function isBirthDateDefinition(definition: FieldDefinition): boolean {
  return definition.kind === 'date' && definition.key === BIRTH_DATE_FIELD_KEY;
}

/** A valid ISO day strictly after `today`. Both sides are civil days compared as strings. */
function isFutureIsoDay(draft: FieldDraft | undefined, today: string): boolean {
  if (typeof draft !== 'string') return false;
  const day = draft.trim();
  return isIsoDay(day) && day > today;
}

function checkNumber(draft: FieldDraft | undefined): string | null {
  if (typeof draft !== 'string' || draft.trim() === '') return null;
  return Number.isFinite(Number(draft)) ? null : LEAD_FORM_COPY.numberInvalid;
}

/**
 * `contactOptional` is set by the caller for an EDIT of a lead that never had a contact
 * (most legacy leads). The API accepts a row without contact, so the form does not force one
 * on it; a new lead, or one that already has a contact, still needs one.
 * `today` is the São Paulo civil day the caller passes (`todayInSaoPaulo()`); this module never reads the clock.
 */
export function validateLeadFormDraft(
  draft: LeadFormDraft,
  definitions: readonly FieldDefinition[],
  options: { contactOptional?: boolean; today: string },
): LeadFormProblem | null {
  if (draft.contactName.trim() === '') return { field: 'contactName', message: LEAD_FORM_COPY.nameRequired };
  if (!options.contactOptional && draft.contactPhone.trim() === '' && draft.contactEmail.trim() === '') {
    return { field: 'contact', message: LEAD_FORM_COPY.contactRequired };
  }
  if (draft.contactEmail.trim() !== '' && !EMAIL_PATTERN.test(draft.contactEmail.trim())) {
    return { field: 'contactEmail', message: LEAD_FORM_COPY.emailInvalid };
  }
  for (const definition of renderableDefinitions(definitions)) {
    const message = FIELD_CHECK_BY_KIND[definition.kind](draft.fields[definition.id]);
    if (message !== null) return { field: `field:${definition.id}`, message };
    if (isBirthDateDefinition(definition) && isFutureIsoDay(draft.fields[definition.id], options.today)) {
      return { field: `field:${definition.id}`, message: LEAD_FORM_COPY.birthDateFuture };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function trimmedOrNull(value: string): string | null {
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  return trimmed === '' ? null : trimmed;
}

const WRITE_BY_KIND: Record<FieldKind, (draft: FieldDraft | undefined) => LeadFieldWriteValue> = {
  text: (draft) => (typeof draft === 'string' && draft.trim() !== '' ? draft.trim() : null),
  options: (draft) => (typeof draft === 'string' && draft.trim() !== '' ? draft.trim() : null),
  date: (draft) => (typeof draft === 'string' && draft.trim() !== '' ? draft.trim() : null),
  number: (draft) => (typeof draft === 'string' && draft.trim() !== '' ? Number(draft) : null),
  money: (draft) => (typeof draft === 'string' && draft.trim() !== '' ? parseCurrencyInputToCents(draft) : null),
  reference: (draft) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(draft)) return null;
    const items = draft.map((item) => ({ id: item.id, name: item.name.trim() })).filter((item) => item.name !== '');
    return items.length > 0 ? items : null;
  },
};

export function fieldDraftToWrite(definition: FieldDefinition, draft: FieldDraft | undefined): LeadFieldWriteValue {
  return WRITE_BY_KIND[definition.kind](draft);
}

export function buildLeadWritePayload(
  draft: LeadFormDraft,
  definitions: readonly FieldDefinition[],
  options: {
    id?: string;
    includeSeller: boolean;
    pipelineId?: string;
    hadValue?: ReadonlySet<string>;
    /** Edit of a contact-less lead that still has none: the contact keys are omitted, not sent as null. */
    contactOptional?: boolean;
  },
): LeadWritePayload {
  const phone = trimmedOrNull(draft.contactPhone);
  const email = trimmedOrNull(draft.contactEmail);
  const omitContact = options.contactOptional === true && phone === null && email === null;
  const fields: LeadFieldWrite[] = [];
  for (const definition of renderableDefinitions(definitions)) {
    const value = fieldDraftToWrite(definition, draft.fields[definition.id]);
    if (value !== null || options.hadValue?.has(definition.id)) fields.push({ fieldId: definition.id, value });
  }
  return {
    ...(options.id ? { id: options.id } : {}),
    contactName: draft.contactName.trim(),
    ...(omitContact ? {} : { contactPhone: phone, contactEmail: email }),
    clientId: draft.clientId,
    clientName: trimmedOrNull(draft.clientName),
    estimatedValueBrl: parseCurrencyInputToCents(draft.estimatedValue),
    ...(options.includeSeller ? { sellerPersonId: draft.sellerPersonId } : {}),
    ...(!options.id && options.pipelineId ? { pipelineId: options.pipelineId } : {}),
    fields,
  };
}

/**
 * The full previous state as a write body, used by the edit undo. The Cliente is
 * carried ONLY by `clientId`/`clientName`: the API refuses `clientId` together with a
 * Cliente entry of `fields` (400 field_invalid), so that entry is dropped here.
 */
export function leadToWriteSeed(lead: SalesOpsLead, clearFieldIds: readonly string[] = []): LeadWritePayload {
  const kept: LeadFieldWrite[] = (lead.fields ?? [])
    .filter((f) => f.key !== CLIENTE_FIELD_KEY)
    .map((f) => ({ fieldId: f.fieldId, value: f.value }));
  return {
    id: lead.id,
    contactName: lead.contactName,
    contactPhone: lead.contactPhone,
    contactEmail: lead.contactEmail,
    clientId: lead.clientId,
    clientName: lead.clientNameSnapshot || null,
    estimatedValueBrl: lead.estimatedValueBrl,
    sellerPersonId: lead.sellerPersonId,
    fields: [...kept, ...clearFieldIds.map((fieldId) => ({ fieldId, value: null }))],
  };
}

export function leadFormSaveErrorCopy(error: unknown): string {
  if (
    typeof error === 'object' &&
    error !== null &&
    (error as { status?: unknown }).status === 400 &&
    (error as { reason?: unknown }).reason === 'no_open_stage'
  ) {
    return LEAD_FORM_COPY.noStageError;
  }
  return LEAD_FORM_COPY.saveFailed;
}

import { SAO_PAULO_TIME_ZONE } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/sao-paulo-day';
import { formatMoneyBrl } from '../calculations';
import { displayDate } from '../civil-day';
import type { FieldDefinition, FieldKind, LeadFieldValue, LeadTimelineItem, LeadTimelineKind } from './crm-types';
import { CLIENTE_FIELD_KEY } from './lead-form';
import type { SalesOpsLead } from './types';

/*
 * Pure formatting of the lead summary page. No React, no clock: every "today" is
 * passed in, and every civil day goes through `displayDate` (never `new Date`).
 */

const INSTANT_FORMAT = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('pt-BR', {
  timeZone: SAO_PAULO_TIME_ZONE,
  day: '2-digit',
  month: '2-digit',
  year: 'numeric',
  hour: '2-digit',
  minute: '2-digit',
  hourCycle: 'h23',
});

/** An instant as `dd/mm/aaaa HH:mm` in America/Sao_Paulo, or '' for an unreadable value. */
export function formatInstantSaoPaulo(iso: string): string {
  const time = Date.parse(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(time)) return '';
  const parts = Object.fromEntries(INSTANT_FORMAT.formatToParts(new Date(time)).map((part) => [part.type, part.value]));
  return `${parts.day}/${parts.month}/${parts.year} ${parts.hour}:${parts.minute}`;
}

export const TIMELINE_LABEL: Record<
  LeadTimelineKind,
  (item: LeadTimelineItem) => { title: string; detail: string | null }
> = {
  stage_changed: (item) => ({
    title: item.fromStageName
      ? `Etapa: ${item.fromStageName} para ${item.toStageName ?? ''}`
      : `Entrou em ${item.toStageName ?? ''}`,
    detail: item.reason ? `Motivo: ${item.reason}` : null,
  }),
  note: (item) => ({ title: 'Nota', detail: item.body ?? null }),
  followup: (item) => ({
    title: `Próximo passo: ${item.nextStep ?? ''}`,
    detail: item.doneAt
      ? `Para ${displayDate(item.dueOn ?? '')} - concluído em ${formatInstantSaoPaulo(item.doneAt)}`
      : `Para ${displayDate(item.dueOn ?? '')}`,
  }),
};

/** Newest first by instant; a stable sort keeps equal instants in the server's order. */
export function sortTimeline(items: readonly LeadTimelineItem[]): LeadTimelineItem[] {
  return [...items].sort((a, b) => (Date.parse(b.at) || 0) - (Date.parse(a.at) || 0));
}

export type FollowupDueState = 'overdue' | 'today' | 'later';

/** Civil-day comparison of a follow-up's due day against today (both `aaaa-mm-dd`). */
export function followupDueState(dueOn: string, today: string): FollowupDueState {
  if (dueOn < today) return 'overdue';
  if (dueOn === today) return 'today';
  return 'later';
}

export const FOLLOWUP_STATE_LABEL: Record<FollowupDueState, string> = {
  overdue: 'Atrasado',
  today: 'Hoje',
  later: 'Agendado',
};

const FIELD_DISPLAY_BY_KIND: Record<FieldKind, (def: FieldDefinition, value: LeadFieldValue['value']) => string> = {
  text: (_def, value) => (typeof value === 'string' || typeof value === 'number' ? String(value) : ''),
  number: (_def, value) => (typeof value === 'string' || typeof value === 'number' ? String(value) : ''),
  money: (_def, value) => {
    const cents = typeof value === 'string' ? Number(value) : value;
    return typeof cents === 'number' && Number.isFinite(cents) ? formatMoneyBrl(cents) : '';
  },
  date: (_def, value) => (typeof value === 'string' ? displayDate(value) : ''),
  options: (def, value) => {
    if (typeof value !== 'string') return '';
    return def.options?.find((option) => option.key === value)?.label ?? value;
  },
  reference: (_def, value) => (Array.isArray(value) ? value.map((item) => item.name).join(', ') : ''),
};

/** The display text of one dynamic value; '' for an empty value. */
export function formatFieldValue(def: FieldDefinition, value: LeadFieldValue['value']): string {
  if (value === null || value === '' || (Array.isArray(value) && value.length === 0)) return '';
  return FIELD_DISPLAY_BY_KIND[def.kind](def, value);
}

/**
 * The dynamic fields a summary shows: the lead's values in definition order, minus
 * the Cliente (it has its own row) and any value that formats to nothing.
 */
export function visibleFieldRows(
  lead: SalesOpsLead,
  definitions: readonly FieldDefinition[],
): Array<{ id: string; label: string; text: string }> {
  const byId = new Map(definitions.map((def) => [def.id, def]));
  const rows: Array<{ def: FieldDefinition; text: string }> = [];
  for (const entry of lead.fields ?? []) {
    const def = byId.get(entry.fieldId);
    if (!def) continue;
    if (def.key === CLIENTE_FIELD_KEY || def.referenceTarget === 'cliente') continue;
    const text = formatFieldValue(def, entry.value);
    if (text !== '') rows.push({ def, text });
  }
  return rows
    .sort((a, b) => a.def.position - b.def.position)
    .map(({ def, text }) => ({ id: def.id, label: def.label, text }));
}

/** The lead summary page. Pure: props in, callbacks out. Never renders an id. */
export function LeadSummary({
  summary,
  definitions,
  today,
  now,
  onBack,
  onEdit,
  onAdvance,
  onOpenSale,
  notesSlot,
  renderFollowupAction,
}: LeadSummaryProps) {
  const { lead, stage, pipeline, nextStages } = summary;
  const converted = leadIsConverted(lead);
  const unassigned = leadIsUnassigned(lead);
  const stageColor = stageColors([{ id: stage.id, kind: legacyKindOf(stage) }]).get(stage.id);
  const openFollowups = summary.followups
    .filter((followup) => followup.doneAt === null)
    .sort((a, b) => (a.dueOn < b.dueOn ? -1 : a.dueOn > b.dueOn ? 1 : 0));
  const fieldRows = visibleFieldRows(lead, definitions);

  return (
    <div data-lead-summary={lead.id} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-2">
          <button className={secondaryButtonClass} data-summary-back onClick={onBack} type="button">
            Voltar
          </button>
          <h2 className="break-words text-[22px] font-semibold text-[#1f1f27]">{lead.contactName}</h2>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[13px]">
            <span
              className="rounded-full border px-2.5 py-0.5 font-semibold"
              data-summary-stage
              style={stageColor ? { background: stageColor.soft, borderColor: stageColor.border, color: stageColor.ink } : undefined}
            >
              {stage.name}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[#8b8b92]">{pipeline.name}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {converted ? (
            lead.saleId && onOpenSale ? (
              <button className={primaryButtonClass} data-summary-open-sale onClick={() => onOpenSale(lead.saleId!)} type="button">
                Abrir proposta{lead.saleCode ? ` ${lead.saleCode}` : ''}
              </button>
            ) : null
          ) : (
            <>
              <button className={secondaryButtonClass} data-summary-edit onClick={onEdit} type="button">
                Editar
              </button>
              {onAdvance ? (
                <button
                  className={primaryButtonClass}
                  data-summary-advance
                  disabled={nextStages.length === 0}
                  onClick={onAdvance}
                  title={nextStages.length === 0 ? NO_ADVANCE : undefined}
                  type="button"
                >
                  Avançar
                </button>
              ) : null}
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </header>

      <section aria-label="Dados" className="rounded-[14px] border border-[#e8e8ec] bg-white p-4" data-summary-section="dados">
        <dl className="flex flex-col">
          <DataRow label="Cliente">{lead.clientNameSnapshot || NO_CLIENT}</DataRow>
          <DataRow label="Contato">{leadContactLine(lead)}</DataRow>
          <DataRow label="Responsável">{unassigned ? UNASSIGNED_LEAD_LABEL : lead.sellerNameSnapshot || 'Sem vendedor'}</DataRow>
          <DataRow label="Valor estimado">{formatMoneyBrl(lead.estimatedValueBrl)}</DataRow>
          <DataRow label="Na etapa há">{`${daysInCurrentStage(lead.stageChangedAt, now)} dias`}</DataRow>
          <DataRow label="Criado em">{displayDate(saoPauloDayOf(new Date(lead.createdAt)))}</DataRow>
          {fieldRows.map((row) => (
            <DataRow key={row.id} label={row.label}>{row.text}</DataRow>
          ))}
        </dl>
      </section>

      <section aria-label="Próximos passos" className="rounded-[14px] border border-[#e8e8ec] bg-white p-4" data-summary-section="followups">
        <h3 className="mb-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-[#1f1f27]">Próximos passos</h3>
        {openFollowups.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-[13px] text-[#8b8b92]">{NO_FOLLOWUPS}</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            {openFollowups.map((followup) => {
              const state = followupDueState(followup.dueOn, today);
              return (
                <li className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 border-t border-[#f0f0f3] pt-2 first:border-t-0" key={followup.id}>
                  <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 break-words text-[14px] text-[#1f1f27]">{followup.nextStep}</span>
                  <span className="text-[13px] text-[#57575f]">{displayDate(followup.dueOn)}</span>
                  <span className="text-[13px] text-[#8b8b92]">{followup.ownerName ?? 'Sem responsável'}</span>
                  <span className="rounded-full bg-[#f4f4f6] px-2 py-0.5 text-[12px] font-semibold text-[#57575f]" data-followup-state={state}>
                    {FOLLOWUP_STATE_LABEL[state]}
                  </span>
                  {renderFollowupAction?.(followup)}
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        )}
      </section>

      {notesSlot}

      <section aria-label="Histórico" className="rounded-[14px] border border-[#e8e8ec] bg-white p-4" data-summary-section="timeline">
        <h3 className="mb-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-[#1f1f27]">Histórico</h3>
        <ol className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {sortTimeline(summary.timeline).map((item, index) => {
            const label = TIMELINE_LABEL[item.kind](item);
            return (
              <li className="border-t border-[#f0f0f3] pt-2 first:border-t-0" data-timeline-kind={item.kind} key={`${item.kind}-${item.at}-${index}`}>
                <p className="text-[14px] text-[#1f1f27]">{label.title}</p>
                {label.detail ? <p className="text-[13px] text-[#57575f]">{label.detail}</p> : null}
                <p className="text-[12px] text-[#8b8b92]">
                  {formatInstantSaoPaulo(item.at)} - {item.actorName ?? ANONYMOUS_AUTHOR}
                </p>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ol>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

 succeeded in 0ms:
import { and, asc, eq, isNull, sql, type SQL } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { getDb } from '../../../db/client.js';
import {
  salesOpsCrmLeadEvents,
  salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups,
  salesOpsCrmLeadNotes,
  salesOpsCrmLeads,
  salesOpsPeople,
  salesOpsPipelineStages,
  salesOpsPipelineTransitions,
  salesOpsPipelines,
} from '../../../db/schema.js';
import { LeadInputError } from '../../crm/lead-input-error.js';
import type {
  AdvanceLeadInput,
  LeadFollowupInput,
  LeadNoteInput,
  ListFollowupsQuery,
} from './lead-schemas.js';
import { recordStageChange, stageTransitionAllowed } from './lead-history.js';
import {
  claimantFor,
  leadIdentityConditions,
  leadSellerCondition,
  liveLeadCondition,
  lockLeadBoard,
  readLeadView,
  renumberStage,
  resolveCallerPersonId,
  resolveLeadScopePredicate,
  resolveSellerPersonId,
  scopeActorName,
  type LeadScope,
  type LeadView,
  type WriteLeadResult,
} from './lead-service.js';
import { withTenant } from './with-tenant.js';

/**
 * Lead history, follow-ups and the Avançar act (phase 2b). Every function opens the caller's tenant
 * transaction, passes the one seller-scope gate and reads leads only through `leadIdentityConditions`
 * (org + live + seller rule), so an out-of-scope lead is `not_found` here exactly as it is on the
 * board. Actor names are snapshotted from the verified token (`scopeActorName`), never from a body.
 */
type Db = ReturnType<typeof getDb>;

/** Position far past any column length: `renumberStage` clamps it, so the card lands at the END. */
const APPEND = 100_000;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Summary
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type TimelineItem = {
  kind: 'stage_changed' | 'note' | 'followup';
  at: string;
  actorName: string | null;
  fromStageName?: string | null;
  toStageName?: string | null;
  reason?: string | null;
  body?: string;
  nextStep?: string;
  dueOn?: string;
  doneAt?: string | null;
};

export type FollowupView = { id: string; nextStep: string; dueOn: string; ownerName: string | null; doneAt: string | null };

export type LeadSummary = {
  lead: LeadView;
  pipeline: { id: string; name: string };
  stage: { id: string; name: string; type: string; exitKind: string | null };
  nextStages: Array<{ id: string; name: string; type: string; exitKind: string | null; requiresReason: boolean }>;
  timeline: TimelineItem[];
  followups: FollowupView[];
};

export type LeadSummaryResult =
  | { ok: true; summary: LeadSummary }
  | { ok: false; reason: 'not_found' | 'seller_person_unmapped' };

const iso = (value: Date | null): string | null => (value ? value.toISOString() : null);

export function getLeadSummary(db: Db, orgId: string, id: string, scope: LeadScope): Promise<LeadSummaryResult> {
  return withTenant(db, orgId, async (tx) => {
    const gate = await resolveLeadScopePredicate(tx, orgId, scope);
    if (!gate.ok) return { ok: false, reason: gate.reason } as const;
    const [row] = await tx
      .select({ id: salesOpsCrmLeads.id, pipelineId: salesOpsCrmLeads.pipelineId, stageId: salesOpsCrmLeads.stageId })
      .from(salesOpsCrmLeads)
      .where(and(...leadIdentityConditions(orgId, id, gate)))
      .limit(1);
    if (!row) return { ok: false, reason: 'not_found' } as const;

    const lead = await readLeadView(tx, orgId, id);
    const [pipeline] = await tx
      .select({ id: salesOpsPipelines.id, name: salesOpsPipelines.name })
      .from(salesOpsPipelines)
      .where(and(eq(salesOpsPipelines.orgId, orgId), eq(salesOpsPipelines.id, row.pipelineId)))
      .limit(1);
    const stages = await tx
      .select()
      .from(salesOpsPipelineStages)
      .where(and(eq(salesOpsPipelineStages.orgId, orgId), eq(salesOpsPipelineStages.pipelineId, row.pipelineId)))
      .orderBy(asc(salesOpsPipelineStages.position), asc(salesOpsPipelineStages.name));
    const current = stages.find((stage) => stage.id === row.stageId)!;
    const nameOf = new Map(stages.map((stage) => [stage.id, stage.name]));

    // Only the stages the explicit graph allows from the current one, and only active ones.
    const edges = await tx
      .select({ toStageId: salesOpsPipelineTransitions.toStageId })
      .from(salesOpsPipelineTransitions)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(salesOpsPipelineTransitions.orgId, orgId),
          eq(salesOpsPipelineTransitions.pipelineId, row.pipelineId),
          eq(salesOpsPipelineTransitions.fromStageId, row.stageId),
        ),
      );
    const reachable = new Set(edges.map((edge) => edge.toStageId));
    const nextStages = stages
      .filter((stage) => reachable.has(stage.id) && stage.status === 'active')
      .map((stage) => ({ id: stage.id, name: stage.name, type: stage.type, exitKind: stage.exitKind, requiresReason: stage.requiresReason }));

    const events = await tx
      .select()
      .from(salesOpsCrmLeadEvents)
      .where(and(eq(salesOpsCrmLeadEvents.orgId, orgId), eq(salesOpsCrmLeadEvents.leadId, id)));
    const notes = await tx
      .select()
      .from(salesOpsCrmLeadNotes)
      .where(and(eq(salesOpsCrmLeadNotes.orgId, orgId), eq(salesOpsCrmLeadNotes.leadId, id)));
    const followupRows = await tx
      .select()
      .from(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups)
      .where(and(eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.orgId, orgId), eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.leadId, id)))
      .orderBy(asc(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.dueOn), asc(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.id));

    const timeline: TimelineItem[] = [
      ...events.map((event) => ({
        kind: 'stage_changed' as const,
        at: event.occurredAt.toISOString(),
        actorName: event.actorNameSnapshot,
        fromStageName: event.fromStageId ? (nameOf.get(event.fromStageId) ?? null) : null,
        toStageName: event.toStageId ? (nameOf.get(event.toStageId) ?? null) : null,
        reason: event.reason,
      })),
      ...notes.map((note) => ({
        kind: 'note' as const,
        at: note.createdAt.toISOString(),
        actorName: note.authorNameSnapshot,
        body: note.body,
      })),
      ...followupRows.map((followup) => ({
        kind: 'followup' as const,
        at: followup.createdAt.toISOString(),
        actorName: followup.createdByName,
        nextStep: followup.nextStep,
        dueOn: followup.dueOn,
        doneAt: iso(followup.doneAt),
      })),
    ].sort((a, b) => a.at.localeCompare(b.at));

    return {
      ok: true,
      summary: {
        lead,
        pipeline: pipeline!,
        stage: { id: current.id, name: current.name, type: current.type, exitKind: current.exitKind },
        nextStages,
        timeline,
        followups: followupRows.map((followup) => ({
          id: followup.id,
          nextStep: followup.nextStep,
          dueOn: followup.dueOn,
          ownerName: followup.ownerNameSnapshot,
          doneAt: iso(followup.doneAt),
        })),
      },
    } as const;
  });
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Avançar
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Moves a lead along an edge of its pipeline graph and records why, in ONE transaction:
 *  - the move (end of the destination column, `stage_changed_at = now()`, the claim of a pool lead),
 *  - exactly one `stage_changed` event (actor name from the token),
 *  - the note (linked to that event) and the follow-up, when given.
 * Same lock order as `moveLead`: scope gate, board lock, then the card row.
 *
 * Refusals: `409 transition_not_allowed` (pair absent, or a stage of another pipeline);
 * `409 sale_required_for_conversion` (the destination opens a proposta, owned by the wizard);
 * `400 followup_required` (a non-exit destination without próximo passo AND data, or only one of the two);
 * `400 reason_required` (the destination requires a reason).
 */
export function advanceLead(
  db: Db,
  orgId: string,
  id: string,
  input: AdvanceLeadInput,
  scope: LeadScope,
): Promise<WriteLeadResult> {
  return withTenant(db, orgId, async (tx) => {
    const gate = await resolveLeadScopePredicate(tx, orgId, scope);
    if (!gate.ok) return { ok: false, reason: gate.reason } as const;
    await lockLeadBoard(tx, orgId);

    const [current] = await tx
      .select()
      .from(salesOpsCrmLeads)
      .where(and(...leadIdentityConditions(orgId, id, gate)))
      .limit(1)
      .for('update');
    if (!current) return { ok: false, reason: 'not_found' } as const;
    if (current.saleId !== null) return { ok: false, reason: 'already_converted' } as const;

    const [destination] = await tx
      .select()
      .from(salesOpsPipelineStages)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(salesOpsPipelineStages.orgId, orgId),
          eq(salesOpsPipelineStages.id, input.toStageId),
          eq(salesOpsPipelineStages.status, 'active'),
        ),
      )
      .limit(1);
    if (
      !destination ||
      destination.pipelineId !== current.pipelineId ||
      destination.id === current.stageId ||
      !(await stageTransitionAllowed(tx, orgId, current.pipelineId, current.stageId, destination.id))
    ) {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'transition_not_allowed' } as const;
    }
    if (destination.type === 'orcamento' && destination.behavior === 'opens_orcamentos') {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'sale_required_for_conversion' } as const;
    }

    const isExit = destination.type === 'saida';
    const hasStep = input.nextStep !== undefined;
    const hasDay = input.dueOn !== undefined;
    if ((!isExit && !(hasStep && hasDay)) || hasStep !== hasDay) throw new LeadInputError('followup_required');
    if (destination.requiresReason && input.reason === undefined) throw new LeadInputError('reason_required');

    const claimant = claimantFor(gate, current);
    const claimed = claimant !== null ? await resolveSellerPersonId(tx, orgId, claimant) : null;

    await tx
      .update(salesOpsCrmLeads)
      .set({
        stageId: destination.id,
        stageChangedAt: new Date(),
        lostReason: destination.exitKind === 'perdido' ? (input.reason ?? null) : null,
        ...(claimed ? { sellerPersonId: claimed.id, sellerNameSnapshot: claimed.displayName } : {}),
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      })
      .where(and(eq(salesOpsCrmLeads.orgId, orgId), eq(salesOpsCrmLeads.id, id), liveLeadCondition()));
    await renumberStage(tx, orgId, destination.id, { moved: id, at: APPEND });
    await renumberStage(tx, orgId, current.stageId, null);

    const actorName = scopeActorName(scope);
    const eventId = await recordStageChange(tx, {
      orgId,
      leadId: id,
      fromStageId: current.stageId,
      toStageId: destination.id,
      reason: input.reason ?? null,
      actorUserId: scope.userId,
      actorName,
    });
    if (input.note !== undefined) {
      await tx.insert(salesOpsCrmLeadNotes).values({
        orgId,
        leadId: id,
        eventId,
        body: input.note,
        authorUserId: scope.userId,
        authorNameSnapshot: actorName,
      });
    }
    if (hasStep && hasDay) {
      await tx.insert(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups).values({
        orgId,
        leadId: id,
        nextStep: input.nextStep!,
        dueOn: input.dueOn!,
        ownerPersonId: claimed?.id ?? current.sellerPersonId,
        ownerNameSnapshot: claimed?.displayName ?? (current.sellerNameSnapshot || null),
        createdByUserId: scope.userId,
        createdByName: actorName,
      });
    }
    return { ok: true, lead: await readLeadView(tx, orgId, id) } as const;
  });
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Notes and follow-ups
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type LeadNoteView = { id: string; body: string; authorName: string | null; createdAt: string };

export type AddNoteResult =
  | { ok: true; note: LeadNoteView }
  | { ok: false; reason: 'not_found' | 'seller_person_unmapped' };

async function inScopeLead(tx: Db, orgId: string, id: string, scope: LeadScope) {
  const gate = await resolveLeadScopePredicate(tx, orgId, scope);
  if (!gate.ok) return { gate, lead: null } as const;
  const [lead] = await tx
    .select({ id: salesOpsCrmLeads.id, sellerPersonId: salesOpsCrmLeads.sellerPersonId, sellerNameSnapshot: salesOpsCrmLeads.sellerNameSnapshot })
    .from(salesOpsCrmLeads)
    .where(and(...leadIdentityConditions(orgId, id, gate)))
    .limit(1);
  return { gate, lead: lead ?? null } as const;
}

export function addLeadNote(db: Db, orgId: string, id: string, input: LeadNoteInput, scope: LeadScope): Promise<AddNoteResult> {
  return withTenant(db, orgId, async (tx) => {
    const { gate, lead } = await inScopeLead(tx, orgId, id, scope);
    if (!gate.ok) return { ok: false, reason: gate.reason } as const;
    if (!lead) return { ok: false, reason: 'not_found' } as const;
    const [note] = await tx
      .insert(salesOpsCrmLeadNotes)
      .values({ orgId, leadId: id, eventId: null, body: input.body, authorUserId: scope.userId, authorNameSnapshot: scopeActorName(scope) })
      .returning();
    return {
      ok: true,
      note: { id: note!.id, body: note!.body, authorName: note!.authorNameSnapshot, createdAt: note!.createdAt.toISOString() },
    } as const;
  });
}

export type AddFollowupResult =
  | { ok: true; followup: FollowupView }
  | { ok: false; reason: 'not_found' | 'seller_person_unmapped' };

const toFollowupView = (row: typeof salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.$inferSelect): FollowupView => ({
  id: row.id,
  nextStep: row.nextStep,
  dueOn: row.dueOn,
  ownerName: row.ownerNameSnapshot,
  doneAt: iso(row.doneAt),
});

export function addLeadFollowup(db: Db, orgId: string, id: string, input: LeadFollowupInput, scope: LeadScope): Promise<AddFollowupResult> {
  return withTenant(db, orgId, async (tx) => {
    const { gate, lead } = await inScopeLead(tx, orgId, id, scope);
    if (!gate.ok) return { ok: false, reason: gate.reason } as const;
    if (!lead) return { ok: false, reason: 'not_found' } as const;
    let ownerId = lead.sellerPersonId;
    let ownerName: string | null = lead.sellerNameSnapshot || null;
    if (input.ownerPersonId) {
      const [owner] = await tx
        .select({ id: salesOpsPeople.id, displayName: salesOpsPeople.displayName })
        .from(salesOpsPeople)
        .where(and(eq(salesOpsPeople.orgId, orgId), eq(salesOpsPeople.id, input.ownerPersonId)))
        .limit(1);
      if (!owner) throw new LeadInputError('owner_not_found');
      ownerId = owner.id;
      ownerName = owner.displayName;
    }
    const [row] = await tx
      .insert(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups)
      .values({
        orgId,
        leadId: id,
        nextStep: input.nextStep,
        dueOn: input.dueOn,
        ownerPersonId: ownerId,
        ownerNameSnapshot: ownerName,
        createdByUserId: scope.userId,
        createdByName: scopeActorName(scope),
      })
      .returning();
    return { ok: true, followup: toFollowupView(row!) } as const;
  });
}

export type DoneResult =
  | { ok: true; followup: FollowupView }
  | { ok: false; reason: 'not_found' | 'seller_person_unmapped' };

/** Idempotent: marking an already-done follow-up keeps its original `done_at`. */
export function markFollowupDone(db: Db, orgId: string, followupId: string, scope: LeadScope): Promise<DoneResult> {
  return withTenant(db, orgId, async (tx) => {
    const gate = await resolveLeadScopePredicate(tx, orgId, scope);
    if (!gate.ok) return { ok: false, reason: gate.reason } as const;
    const [row] = await tx
      .select({ followup: salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups })
      .from(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups)
      .innerJoin(
        salesOpsCrmLeads,
        and(eq(salesOpsCrmLeads.orgId, salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.orgId), eq(salesOpsCrmLeads.id, salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.leadId)),
      )
      .where(
        and(
          eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.orgId, orgId),
          eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.id, followupId),
          liveLeadCondition(),
          ...(gate.sellerPersonId ? [leadSellerCondition(gate.sellerPersonId, gate.canClaimUnassigned)] : []),
        ),
      )
      .limit(1)
      .for('update', { of: salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups });
    if (!row) return { ok: false, reason: 'not_found' } as const;
    if (row.followup.doneAt !== null) return { ok: true, followup: toFollowupView(row.followup) } as const;
    const [done] = await tx
      .update(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups)
      .set({ doneAt: new Date(), updatedAt: new Date() })
      .where(and(eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.orgId, orgId), eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.id, followupId)))
      .returning();
    return { ok: true, followup: toFollowupView(done!) } as const;
  });
}

export type FollowupListRow = {
  id: string;
  leadId: string;
  leadContactName: string;
  nextStep: string;
  dueOn: string;
  ownerName: string | null;
  doneAt: string | null;
};

export type ListFollowupsResult =
  | { ok: true; followups: FollowupListRow[] }
  | { ok: false; reason: 'seller_person_unmapped' };

/**
 * `mine`: the caller's pessoa owns it, or the caller created it. `team`: every follow-up of the org
 * (the route admits only an admin). The period is on `due_on`, a civil `date`, so the days are
 * compared as written and no timezone is involved. Deleted leads' follow-ups are not listed.
 */
export function listFollowups(db: Db, orgId: string, query: ListFollowupsQuery, scope: LeadScope): Promise<ListFollowupsResult> {
  return withTenant(db, orgId, async (tx) => {
    const conditions: SQL[] = [eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.orgId, orgId), liveLeadCondition()];
    if (query.scope === 'mine') {
      const personId = await resolveCallerPersonId(tx, orgId, scope);
      const mine: SQL[] = [eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.createdByUserId, scope.userId)];
      if (personId) mine.push(eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.ownerPersonId, personId));
      conditions.push(mine.length === 1 ? mine[0]! : sql`(${mine[0]} OR ${mine[1]})`);
    }
    if (query.status === 'open') conditions.push(isNull(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.doneAt));
    if (query.status === 'done') conditions.push(sql`${salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.doneAt} IS NOT NULL`);
    if (query.from) conditions.push(sql`${salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.dueOn} >= ${query.from}::date`);
    if (query.to) conditions.push(sql`${salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.dueOn} <= ${query.to}::date`);
    const rows = await tx
      .select({ followup: salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups, contactName: salesOpsCrmLeads.contactName })
      .from(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups)
      .innerJoin(
        salesOpsCrmLeads,
        and(eq(salesOpsCrmLeads.orgId, salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.orgId), eq(salesOpsCrmLeads.id, salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.leadId)),
      )
      .where(and(...conditions))
      .orderBy(asc(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.dueOn), asc(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.id))
      .limit(query.limit);
    return {
      ok: true,
      followups: rows.map(({ followup, contactName }) => ({
        id: followup.id,
        leadId: followup.leadId,
        leadContactName: contactName,
        nextStep: followup.nextStep,
        dueOn: followup.dueOn,
        ownerName: followup.ownerNameSnapshot,
        doneAt: iso(followup.doneAt),
      })),
    } as const;
  });
}

394:## CRM unificado
396:Full reference: `nexo/knowledge/reference/kanban-de-leads.md` (section CRM unificado, API), `nexo/knowledge/decisions/2026-10-21-crm-unificado-modelo.md`.
398:- Leads, etapas, history, notes and follow-ups live in the unified tables (`sales_ops_crm_leads`, `sales_ops_pipeline_stages`, `sales_ops_crm_lead_events`, `..._notes`, `..._followups`, `sales_ops_crm_lead_field_values`). `sales_ops_leads`, `sales_ops_lead_products` and `sales_ops_lead_stages` are a FROZEN copy: no product code reads or writes them (`frozen-lead-tables.test.ts`, `crm-cutover-frozen-tables.test.ts`).
399:- A pipeline is a flowchart: etapas of `type` `normal | orcamento | saida` (exit `ganho | perdido | pausado`) and the explicit `sales_ops_pipeline_transitions` graph; a pair that is absent is not allowed. A cross-stage `POST /leads/:id/move` or `POST /leads/:id/advance` outside the graph answers `409 {"error":"transition_not_allowed"}` (a stage of another pipeline included); a reorder inside one column is always allowed. The spreadsheet import places historical leads with `skipTransitionGraph`.
401:- `POST /leads/:id/advance { toStageId, nextStep?, dueOn?, note?, reason? }` follows the lock order of `moveLead`; `400 followup_required` unless the destination is an exit (and a half follow-up is refused even on an exit), `400 reason_required` when the destination requires one, `409 sale_required_for_conversion` for an etapa that opens a proposta (the wizard and `POST /sales` own that conversion, and the lead's `sale_id` is still set by `moveLead`).
402:- Follow-ups: `GET /followups?scope=mine|team&from&to&status=open|done|all` (`team` is admin only, the shared 403 body), `POST /leads/:id/followups`, `POST /followups/:id/done` (idempotent). Their period is on `due_on`, a civil `date`, compared as written.
403:- Period filters on a timestamp column go through `saoPauloDayRangeToInstants` (`@fxl-sales/shared-utils/sao-paulo-day`): `[from 00:00 -03:00, to+1 00:00 -03:00)`, so a lead created at 02:30Z on day D+1 belongs to day D. Never `slice(0, 10)` of an instant.
404:- `GET /leads` and `GET /leads/summary` accept `pipelineId`, `from`, `to` and `f.<fieldKey>[.min|.max]` for FILTERABLE definitions only (cliente by id, options by key, text contains, number/money/date by min/max); an unknown, non-filterable or ill-typed key is `400 {"error":"validation_error","reason":"invalid_filter"}`. The summary defaults to the org's default pipeline.
406:- Tests that create CRM rows delete them with `deleteCrmForOrgs` BEFORE deleting clientes, pessoas, produtos or propostas.
438:- Both routes seed the default etapas (`ensureLeadStages`) and the system funções (`ensureSystemFuncoes`) inside their transaction before `readImportCatalog`. `readImportCatalog` and the executor never seed.
441:- A lead cannot be imported into the conversion etapa; one in the lost etapa needs `Motivo da perda`.

import type { CrmPipeline, CrmPipelineStage, PipelineExitKind, PipelineStageType } from './crm-types';
import type { LeadStageKind, SalesOpsLeadStage } from './types';

export const OPENS_ORCAMENTOS_BEHAVIOR = 'opens_orcamentos';

export const LEGACY_KIND_BY_TYPE: Record<PipelineStageType, LeadStageKind> = {
  normal: 'normal',
  orcamento: 'conversion',
  saida: 'normal',
};

export const LEGACY_KIND_BY_EXIT: Record<PipelineExitKind, LeadStageKind> = {
  ganho: 'normal',
  perdido: 'lost',
  pausado: 'normal',
};

export function legacyKindOf(stage: Pick<CrmPipelineStage, 'type' | 'exitKind'>): LeadStageKind {
  return stage.exitKind ? LEGACY_KIND_BY_EXIT[stage.exitKind] : LEGACY_KIND_BY_TYPE[stage.type];
}

/** Selected pipeline, else the default one, else the first (position order). undefined when there is none. */
export function pickPipeline(pipelines: readonly CrmPipeline[], selectedId: string | null): CrmPipeline | undefined {
  const active = pipelines.filter((p) => p.status === 'active');
  return active.find((p) => p.id === selectedId) ?? active.find((p) => p.isDefault) ?? active[0];
}

/** One board stage per ACTIVE pipeline stage, in position order, with the legacy kind filled by lookup. */
export function pipelineStagesAsBoardStages(pipeline: CrmPipeline | undefined): SalesOpsLeadStage[] {
  if (!pipeline) return [];
  return [...pipeline.stages]
    .filter((s) => s.status === 'active')
    .sort((a, b) => a.position - b.position || a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'pt-BR'))
    .map((s) => ({
      id: s.id,
      orgId: '',
      name: s.name,
      position: s.position,
      kind: legacyKindOf(s),
      isSystem: s.type !== 'normal',
      status: s.status,
      archivedAt: null,
      createdAt: '',
      updatedAt: null,
      pipelineId: pipeline.id,
      type: s.type,
      behavior: s.behavior,
      exitKind: s.exitKind,
      requiresReason: s.requiresReason,
    }));
}

/** A stage always "allows" itself (reorder inside the column). */
export function isTransitionAllowed(pipeline: CrmPipeline | undefined, fromStageId: string, toStageId: string): boolean {
  if (fromStageId === toStageId) return true;
  if (!pipeline) return false;
  return pipeline.transitions.some((t) => t.fromStageId === fromStageId && t.toStageId === toStageId);
}

export function allowedTargetIds(pipeline: CrmPipeline | undefined, fromStageId: string): Set<string> {
  const ids = new Set<string>([fromStageId]);
  for (const t of pipeline?.transitions ?? []) if (t.fromStageId === fromStageId) ids.add(t.toStageId);
  return ids;
}

export function stageOpensOrcamentos(stage: Pick<CrmPipelineStage, 'behavior'> | undefined): boolean {
  return stage?.behavior === OPENS_ORCAMENTOS_BEHAVIOR;
}
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useAccessToken } from '@/auth/react';
import { useAppMutation } from '@/lib/app-mutation';
import { queryKeys } from '@/lib/query-keys';
import { requireToken } from '@/lib/require-token';
import { crmApi } from './crm-api';
import type {
  AddLeadFollowupPayload,
  AddLeadNotePayload,
  AdvanceLeadPayload,
  CrmPipeline,
  FieldDefinition,
  FieldDefinitionsResponse,
  FollowupListRow,
  FollowupsResponse,
  LeadSummaryResponse,
  ListFollowupsParams,
  PipelinesResponse,
} from './crm-types';
import type { LeadResponse } from './types';

/*
 * The READ hooks of the CRM layer. The mutation hooks join this file in 09c and 09d
 * through `useAppMutation`: `useMutation` stays banned outside `app-mutation`.
 */

function selectPipelines(data: PipelinesResponse): CrmPipeline[] {
  return Array.isArray(data.pipelines) ? data.pipelines : [];
}

function selectFieldDefinitions(data: FieldDefinitionsResponse): FieldDefinition[] {
  return Array.isArray(data.fields) ? data.fields : [];
}

export function usePipelines() {
  const { getToken } = useAccessToken();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.leads.pipelines(),
    queryFn: async () => crmApi.listPipelines(await requireToken(getToken)),
    select: selectPipelines,
  });
}

export function useFieldDefinitions() {
  const { getToken } = useAccessToken();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.leads.fieldDefinitions(),
    queryFn: async () => crmApi.listFieldDefinitions(await requireToken(getToken)),
    select: selectFieldDefinitions,
  });
}

/**
 * The lead summary (`GET /leads/:id/summary`). No `retry` override: a 404 must
 * reach the container at once so it can answer `Lead não encontrado.`.
 */
export function useLeadSummary(leadId: string) {
  const { getToken } = useAccessToken();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.leads.detail(leadId),
    queryFn: async (): Promise<LeadSummaryResponse> => crmApi.getLeadSummary(leadId, await requireToken(getToken)),
  });
}

/*
 * The CRM writes. Each refreshes the whole `['leads']` root, so the summary, the
 * board and the follow-ups list re-read the server. No optimistic write: the server
 * owns the next stage, the position and the follow-up rows.
 */
export function useAdvanceLead() {
  const { getToken } = useAccessToken();
  return useAppMutation<LeadResponse, Error, AdvanceLeadPayload>({
    mutationFn: async (payload) => crmApi.advanceLead(payload, await requireToken(getToken)),
    invalidates: [queryKeys.leads.all],
  });
}

export function useAddLeadNote() {
  const { getToken } = useAccessToken();
  return useAppMutation<unknown, Error, AddLeadNotePayload>({
    mutationFn: async (payload) => crmApi.addLeadNote(payload, await requireToken(getToken)),
    invalidates: [queryKeys.leads.all],
  });
}

export function useAddLeadFollowup() {
  const { getToken } = useAccessToken();
  return useAppMutation<unknown, Error, AddLeadFollowupPayload>({
    mutationFn: async (payload) => crmApi.addLeadFollowup(payload, await requireToken(getToken)),
    invalidates: [queryKeys.leads.all],
  });
}

/** The variable is the follow-up id. */
export function useMarkFollowupDone() {
  const { getToken } = useAccessToken();
  return useAppMutation<unknown, Error, string>({
    mutationFn: async (followupId) => crmApi.markFollowupDone(followupId, await requireToken(getToken)),
    invalidates: [queryKeys.leads.all],
  });
}

function selectFollowups(data: FollowupsResponse): FollowupListRow[] {
  return Array.isArray(data.followups) ? data.followups : [];
}

/** The follow-ups list for one scope, status and period (`GET /followups`). */
export function useFollowups(params: ListFollowupsParams) {
  const { getToken } = useAccessToken();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.leads.followups({
      scope: params.scope,
      status: params.status,
      ...(params.from ? { from: params.from } : {}),
      ...(params.to ? { to: params.to } : {}),
    }),
    queryFn: async () => crmApi.listFollowups(params, await requireToken(getToken)),
    select: selectFollowups,
  });
}

import * as React from 'react';
import { useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { todayInSaoPaulo } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/sao-paulo-day';
import { useAuthProfile } from '@/auth/react';
import type { ComboboxOption } from '@/components/ui/combobox';
import { Skeleton } from '@/components/ui/skeleton';
import { buildSalesOpsPath, type SalesOpsWorkspace } from '../navigation';
import type { SalesOpsClient, SalesOpsProduct } from '../types';
import type { LeadWritePayload } from './api';
import { AdvanceDialog } from './AdvanceDialog';
import { useSaveLead, useDeleteLead, useMoveLead } from './hooks';
import {
  useAddLeadFollowup,
  useAddLeadNote,
  useAdvanceLead,
  useFieldDefinitions,
  useLeadSummary,
  useMarkFollowupDone,
  usePipelines,
} from './crm-hooks';
import { leadIsConverted } from './calculations';
import { pickPipeline, stageOpensOrcamentos } from './crm-pipeline';
import { mutedStateClass, secondaryButtonClass } from './board-ui';
import { LeadDeleteDialog } from './LeadDeleteDialog';
import { LeadForm } from './LeadForm';
import { CLIENTE_FIELD_KEY, leadToWriteSeed } from './lead-form';
import type { LeadConversionRequest } from './LeadsBoard';
import { LeadSummary } from './LeadSummary';

import { FollowupComposer, NoteComposer } from './SummaryComposers';

import { UNDO_CONFLICT, UndoConflictError } from './undo-conflict';
import { useUndoNotice } from './use-undo-notice';

/*
 * The lead summary route (`/:workspace/leads/:leadId`). The ONLY file of the
 * summary that names hooks; `LeadSummary` is props in, callbacks out.
 */

const LEAD_NOT_FOUND = 'Lead não encontrado.';
const LOAD_FAILED = 'Não foi possível carregar o lead.';

export type LeadSummaryContainerProps = {
  leadId: string;
  workspace: SalesOpsWorkspace;
  clients: SalesOpsClient[];
  products: SalesOpsProduct[];
  sellers: ComboboxOption[];
  onCreateClient?: (name: string) => Promise<ComboboxOption | null>;
  onOpenSale?: (saleId: string) => void;
  /** The conversion door of the board, reused: a conversion destination opens the proposta wizard. */
  onRequestConversion?: (request: LeadConversionRequest) => Promise<string | null>;
};

function isNotFoundError(error: unknown): boolean {
  return typeof error === 'object' && error !== null && (error as { status?: unknown }).status === 404;
}

export function LeadSummaryContainer({
  leadId,
  workspace,
  clients,
  products,
  sellers,
  onCreateClient,
  onOpenSale,
  onRequestConversion,
}: LeadSummaryContainerProps) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const isAdmin = useAuthProfile().roles.includes('admin');
  const summaryQuery = useLeadSummary(leadId);
  const definitionsQuery = useFieldDefinitions();
  const saveLead = useSaveLead();
  const deleteLead = useDeleteLead();
  const undo = useUndoNotice();
  const advance = useAdvanceLead();
  const addNote = useAddLeadNote();
  const addFollowup = useAddLeadFollowup();
  const markDone = useMarkFollowupDone();
  const moveLead = useMoveLead(undefined);
  const pipelinesQuery = usePipelines();
  const [editOpen, setEditOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const [deleteOpen, setDeleteOpen] = React.useState(false);
  const [advanceOpen, setAdvanceOpen] = React.useState(false);
  // Stable per mount, as on the board: the day counters need days, not milliseconds.
  const today = React.useMemo(() => todayInSaoPaulo(), []);
  const now = React.useMemo(() => new Date(), []);

  const summary = summaryQuery.data;
  const lead = summary?.lead;
  const definitions = definitionsQuery.data ?? [];
  const backPath = buildSalesOpsPath({ workspace, view: 'funil' });
  const pipeline = React.useMemo(
    () => pickPipeline(pipelinesQuery.data ?? [], summaryQuery.data?.pipeline.id ?? null),
    [pipelinesQuery.data, summaryQuery.data?.pipeline.id],
  );
  // The destinations that open the proposta: the same set the board's conversion door uses.
  const opensConversionIds = React.useMemo(
    () => new Set((pipeline?.stages ?? []).filter((stage) => stageOpensOrcamentos(stage)).map((stage) => stage.id)),
    [pipeline],
  );

  function goBack() {
    if (isLeadSummaryOpenedInApp(location.state)) navigate(-1);
    else navigate(backPath, { replace: true });
  }

  if (summaryQuery.isPending) {
    return <Skeleton className="h-[320px] w-full" />;
  }

  if (summaryQuery.isError || !summary || !lead) {
    const notFound = isNotFoundError(summaryQuery.error);
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-3">
        <p className={mutedStateClass}>{notFound ? LEAD_NOT_FOUND : LOAD_FAILED}</p>
        <button className={secondaryButtonClass} onClick={goBack} type="button">
          Voltar
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  /*
    The inverse of an edit is only offered while the lead is still what this edit
    left: a fresh read must equal the response to the forward write (the board's rule).
  */
  async function requireUnchangedLead(expected: SalesOpsLead) {
    const fresh = await summaryQuery.refetch({ throwOnError: true });
    if (JSON.stringify(fresh.data?.lead) !== JSON.stringify(expected)) {
      throw new UndoConflictError(UNDO_CONFLICT);
    }
  }

  async function saveWithUndo(payload: LeadWritePayload) {
    const previous = lead!;
    const result = await saveLead.mutateAsync(payload);
    // The Cliente travels as clientId, never as a field entry, so it never counts as "added".
    const previousIds = new Set((previous.fields ?? []).map((f) => f.fieldId));
    const added = (result.lead.fields ?? [])
      .filter((f) => f.key !== CLIENTE_FIELD_KEY && !previousIds.has(f.fieldId))
      .map((f) => f.fieldId);
    undo.publish('Lead editado', async () => {
      await requireUnchangedLead(result.lead);
      return saveLead.mutateAsync(leadToWriteSeed(previous, added));
    });
  }

  /** Resolves `true` once the proposta exists and the lead has moved; `false` when the operator cancelled the wizard. */
  async function convert(stage: { id: string }): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!onRequestConversion) throw new Error('conversion_unavailable');
    const saleId = await onRequestConversion({ lead: lead!, toStageId: stage.id, toIndex: 0 });
    if (saleId === null) return false;
    await moveLead.mutateAsync({ leadId: lead!.id, toStageId: stage.id, toIndex: 0, saleId });
    return true;
  }

  async function confirmDelete(target: SalesOpsLead) {
    await deleteLead.mutateAsync(target.id);
    // The lead is gone and this page with it, so the restore notice would die here:
    // the lead stays restorable from Cadastros > Leads excluídos.
    navigate(backPath, { replace: true });
  }

  return (
    <>
      <LeadSummary
        definitions={definitions}
        now={now}
        notesSlot={(
          <>
            <NoteComposer onAdd={(body) => addNote.mutateAsync({ leadId: lead.id, body })} pending={addNote.isPending} />
            <FollowupComposer
              onAdd={(followup) => addFollowup.mutateAsync({ leadId: lead.id, ...followup })}
              pending={addFollowup.isPending}
              today={today}
            />
          </>
        )}
        onAdvance={leadIsConverted(lead) ? undefined : () => setAdvanceOpen(true)}
        onBack={goBack}
        onEdit={() => setEditOpen(true)}
        onOpenSale={onOpenSale}
        renderFollowupAction={(followup) => (
          <button
            className={secondaryButtonClass}
            data-followup-done={followup.id}
            onClick={() => markDone.mutate(followup.id)}
            type="button"
          >
            Concluir
          </button>
        )}
        summary={summary}
        today={today}
      />
      {advanceOpen ? (
        <AdvanceDialog
          key="advance"
          leadName={lead.contactName}
          nextStages={summary.nextStages}
          onConvert={convert}
          onOpenChange={setAdvanceOpen}
          onSubmit={(payload) => advance.mutateAsync({ leadId: lead.id, ...payload })}
          open
          opensConversionIds={opensConversionIds}
          today={today}
        />
      ) : null}
      {editOpen && !definitionsQuery.isPending ? (
        <LeadForm
          clients={clients}
          definitions={definitions}
          initial={lead}
          key={lead.id}
          onCreateClient={onCreateClient}
          onDelete={leadIsConverted(lead) ? undefined : () => setDeleteOpen(true)}
          onOpenChange={setEditOpen}
          onSubmit={saveWithUndo}
          open
          pending={saveLead.isPending}
          products={products}
          sellers={sellers}
          showSellerPicker={isAdmin}
        />
      ) : null}
      {deleteOpen ? (
        <LeadDeleteDialog
          key={`delete:${lead.id}`}
          lead={lead}
          onConfirm={confirmDelete}
          onOpenChange={(next) => {
            if (!next) setDeleteOpen(false);
          }}
          open
        />
      ) : null}
      <UndoLayer undo={undo} />
    </>
  );
}
