import { isIsoDay } from '@fxl-sales/shared-utils/sao-paulo-day';
import type { AdvanceLeadPayload, LeadNextStage, PipelineStageType } from './crm-types';

/*
 * The pure rules of "Avançar". The dialog draws them and the API enforces them;
 * nothing here reads the clock (`today` is passed in) or the network.
 */

export const ADVANCE_COPY = {
  title: 'Avançar lead',
  stageLabel: 'Etapa de destino',
  nextStepLabel: 'Próximo passo',
  dueOnLabel: 'Data do próximo passo',
  noteLabel: 'Nota (opcional)',
  reasonLabel: 'Motivo',
  confirm: 'Avançar',
  conversionHint: 'Esta etapa abre a proposta. Você preenche a proposta na próxima tela.',
  noDestination: 'Escolha a etapa de destino.',
  nextStepRequired: 'Informe o próximo passo.',
  dueOnRequired: 'Informe a data do próximo passo.',
  dueOnPast: 'A data não pode ser anterior a hoje.',
  reasonRequired: 'Informe o motivo.',
  notAllowed: 'Esse avanço não é permitido neste funil.',
  conversionNeeded: 'Esta etapa exige a proposta. Use o fluxo da proposta.',
  failed: 'Não foi possível avançar o lead. Tente novamente.',
} as const;

export type AdvanceDraft = {
  toStageId: string | null;
  nextStep: string;
  dueOn: string;
  note: string;
  reason: string;
};

/** Every destination type but `saida` (an exit) needs a follow-up, unless it opens the proposta. */
export const FOLLOWUP_REQUIRED_BY_TYPE: Record<PipelineStageType, boolean> = {
  normal: true,
  orcamento: true,
  saida: false,
};

export type AdvanceNeeds = { followup: boolean; reason: boolean; conversion: boolean };

export function advanceNeeds(dest: LeadNextStage | undefined, opensConversion: boolean): AdvanceNeeds {
  if (!dest) return { followup: false, reason: false, conversion: false };
  const conversion = opensConversion;
  return {
    conversion,
    followup: !conversion && FOLLOWUP_REQUIRED_BY_TYPE[dest.type],
    reason: dest.requiresReason,
  };
}

/** The first blocking message for the draft, or null when "Avançar" may be sent. */
export function validateAdvance(
  dest: LeadNextStage | undefined,
  opensConversion: boolean,
  draft: AdvanceDraft,
  today: string,
): string | null {
  if (!dest) return ADVANCE_COPY.noDestination;
  const needs = advanceNeeds(dest, opensConversion);
  // A conversion is not a write of this form: the proposta wizard owns it.
  if (needs.conversion) return null;
  if (needs.reason && draft.reason.trim() === '') return ADVANCE_COPY.reasonRequired;
  if (needs.followup) {
    if (draft.nextStep.trim() === '') return ADVANCE_COPY.nextStepRequired;
    if (draft.dueOn === '' || !isIsoDay(draft.dueOn)) return ADVANCE_COPY.dueOnRequired;
    if (draft.dueOn < today) return ADVANCE_COPY.dueOnPast;
  }
  return null;
}

/** The body of `POST /leads/:id/advance`; the caller adds `leadId`. Only the fields the destination needs are sent. */
export function buildAdvancePayload(
  toStageId: string,
  draft: AdvanceDraft,
  needs: AdvanceNeeds,
): Omit<AdvanceLeadPayload, 'leadId'> {
  const payload: Omit<AdvanceLeadPayload, 'leadId'> = { toStageId };
  if (needs.followup) {
    payload.nextStep = draft.nextStep.trim();
    payload.dueOn = draft.dueOn;
  }
  if (needs.reason) payload.reason = draft.reason.trim();
  const note = draft.note.trim();
  if (note !== '') payload.note = note;
  return payload;
}

/** The copy of an advance refusal. Structural on `{status, error, reason}`, never on a class. */
export function advanceErrorCopy(error: unknown): string {
  const shape = (typeof error === 'object' && error !== null ? error : {}) as {
    status?: unknown;
    error?: unknown;
    reason?: unknown;
  };
  if (shape.status === 409 && shape.error === 'transition_not_allowed') return ADVANCE_COPY.notAllowed;
  if (shape.status === 409 && shape.error === 'sale_required_for_conversion') return ADVANCE_COPY.conversionNeeded;
  if (shape.status === 400 && shape.reason === 'followup_required') return ADVANCE_COPY.nextStepRequired;
  if (shape.status === 400 && shape.reason === 'reason_required') return ADVANCE_COPY.reasonRequired;
  return ADVANCE_COPY.failed;
}
import * as React from 'react';
import { Combobox } from '@/components/ui/combobox';
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
} from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import {
  ADVANCE_COPY,
  type AdvanceDraft,
  advanceErrorCopy,
  advanceNeeds,
  buildAdvancePayload,
  validateAdvance,
} from './advance';
import {
  blockedNoticeClass,
  fieldLabelClass,
  formInputClass,
  formSelectClass,
  formTextareaClass,
  noticeClass,
  primaryButtonClass,
  secondaryButtonClass,
} from './board-ui';
import type { AdvanceLeadPayload, LeadNextStage } from './crm-types';

export type AdvanceDialogProps = {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  leadName: string;
  /** Only the destinations the API allows from this stage. Nothing else is offered. */
  nextStages: LeadNextStage[];
  /** The ids of the destinations that open the proposta (the conversion door). */
  opensConversionIds: ReadonlySet<string>;
  /** The São Paulo civil day, passed in. */
  today: string;
  onSubmit: (payload: Omit<AdvanceLeadPayload, 'leadId'>) => Promise<unknown>;
  /** Resolves `true` when the conversion was carried out, `false` when the operator cancelled it. */
  onConvert: (stage: LeadNextStage) => Promise<boolean>;
};

const EMPTY_DRAFT: AdvanceDraft = { toStageId: null, nextStep: '', dueOn: '', note: '', reason: '' };

/*
 * The "Avançar" dialog. Mount-scoped state, like `MoveLeadDialog`: the container
 * keys it by the open cycle, so a cancelled advance leaves nothing behind.
 */
export function AdvanceDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  leadName,
  nextStages,
  opensConversionIds,
  today,
  onSubmit,
  onConvert,
}: AdvanceDialogProps) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = React.useState<AdvanceDraft>(EMPTY_DRAFT);
  const [touched, setTouched] = React.useState(false);
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = React.useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = React.useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = React.useState<string | null>(null);

  const dest = nextStages.find((stage) => stage.id === draft.toStageId);
  const opensConversion = dest ? opensConversionIds.has(dest.id) : false;
  const needs = advanceNeeds(dest, opensConversion);
  const refusal = validateAdvance(dest, opensConversion, draft, today);
  const showRefusal = (touched || submitted) && refusal !== null;

  function update(patch: Partial<AdvanceDraft>) {
    setTouched(true);
    setDraft((current) => ({ ...current, ...patch }));
  }

  async function confirm() {
    setSubmitted(true);
    if (refusal !== null || !dest) return;
    setSubmitting(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      if (needs.conversion) {
        const done = await onConvert(dest);
        if (!done) return;
      } else {
        await onSubmit(buildAdvancePayload(dest.id, draft, needs));
      }
      onOpenChange(false);
    } catch (caught: unknown) {
      setError(advanceErrorCopy(caught));
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Dialog onOpenChange={onOpenChange} open={open}>
      <DialogContent className="max-w-md">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>{ADVANCE_COPY.title}</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>{`Escolha a próxima etapa de ${leadName}.`}</DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <span className={fieldLabelClass} id="advance-stage-label">
              {ADVANCE_COPY.stageLabel}
            </span>
            <Combobox
              aria-label="Etapa de destino"
              className={formSelectClass}
              onChange={(value) => update({ toStageId: value })}
              options={nextStages.map((stage) => ({ value: stage.id, label: stage.name }))}
              placeholder="Selecione a etapa"
              value={draft.toStageId}
            />
          </div>

          {needs.conversion ? (
            <p className={noticeClass} data-advance-conversion-hint="true">
              {ADVANCE_COPY.conversionHint}
            </p>
          ) : null}

          {needs.followup ? (
            <>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <label className={fieldLabelClass} htmlFor="advance-next-step">
                  {ADVANCE_COPY.nextStepLabel}
                </label>
                <Input
                  className={formInputClass}
                  id="advance-next-step"
                  maxLength={200}
                  onChange={(event) => update({ nextStep: event.target.value })}
                  value={draft.nextStep}
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <label className={fieldLabelClass} htmlFor="advance-due-on">
                  {ADVANCE_COPY.dueOnLabel}
                </label>
                <Input
                  className={formInputClass}
                  id="advance-due-on"
                  min={today}
                  onChange={(event) => update({ dueOn: event.target.value })}
                  type="date"
                  value={draft.dueOn}
                />
              </div>
            </>
          ) : null}

          {needs.reason ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
              <label className={fieldLabelClass} htmlFor="advance-reason">
                {ADVANCE_COPY.reasonLabel}
              </label>
              <textarea
                aria-required="true"
                className={formTextareaClass}
                id="advance-reason"
                onChange={(event) => update({ reason: event.target.value })}
                required
                value={draft.reason}
              />
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {needs.conversion ? null : (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
              <label className={fieldLabelClass} htmlFor="advance-note">
                {ADVANCE_COPY.noteLabel}
              </label>
              <textarea
                className={formTextareaClass}
                id="advance-note"
                onChange={(event) => update({ note: event.target.value })}
                value={draft.note}
              />
            </div>
          )}

          {showRefusal ? (
            <p className={blockedNoticeClass} data-advance-blocked="true">
              {refusal}
            </p>
          ) : null}
          {error ? (
            <p className={blockedNoticeClass} data-advance-error="true" role="alert">
              {error}
            </p>
          ) : null}
        </div>

        <DialogFooter>
          <button className={secondaryButtonClass} onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)} type="button">
            Cancelar
          </button>
          {/* `type="button"` on every render: the activation behaviour must never depend on state. */}
          <button
            className={primaryButtonClass}
            data-advance-confirm="true"
            disabled={refusal !== null || submitting}
            onClick={() => void confirm()}
            type="button"
          >
            {ADVANCE_COPY.confirm}
          </button>
        </DialogFooter>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

/**
 * CRM unificado: the shared vocabulary of the unified lead model (migration 0029).
 *
 * Pure: imports only sibling pure modules, reads no env, no clock, no locale.
 * Open-ended sets (behavior, field kind, reference target, module key, event kind,
 * source) are validated HERE and by zod, never by a database CHECK, because the
 * additive migration guard forbids DROP CONSTRAINT.
 */
import {
  DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGES,
  type DefaultPipelineStage,
  type PipelineExitKind,
  type PipelineStageType,
} from './default-pipeline.js';
import { hasCapability, type SalesCapability, type SalesEdition } from './sales-edition.js';

export const CRM_STATUSES = ['active', 'archived'] as const;
export type CrmStatus = (typeof CRM_STATUSES)[number];

export const PIPELINE_STAGE_TYPES = ['normal', 'orcamento', 'saida'] as const satisfies readonly PipelineStageType[];
export const PIPELINE_EXIT_KINDS = ['ganho', 'perdido', 'pausado'] as const satisfies readonly PipelineExitKind[];

export const STAGE_BEHAVIORS = ['none', 'opens_orcamentos'] as const;
export type StageBehavior = (typeof STAGE_BEHAVIORS)[number];

export const FIELD_KINDS = ['text', 'number', 'money', 'date', 'options', 'reference'] as const;
export type FieldKind = (typeof FIELD_KINDS)[number];

export const FIELD_REFERENCE_TARGETS = ['cliente', 'contato', 'produto', 'servico', 'venda'] as const;
export type FieldReferenceTarget = (typeof FIELD_REFERENCE_TARGETS)[number];

export const LEAD_EVENT_KINDS = ['stage_changed'] as const;
export type LeadEventKind = (typeof LEAD_EVENT_KINDS)[number];

export const CRM_MODULE_KEYS = [
  'orcamentos',
  'comissoes',
  'financeiro',
  'finders',
  'catalogo',
  'importacao',
  'clientes',
] as const;
export type CrmModuleKey = (typeof CRM_MODULE_KEYS)[number];

export const CRM_MODULE_SOURCES = ['default', 'token_backfill', 'admin'] as const;
export type CrmModuleSource = (typeof CRM_MODULE_SOURCES)[number];

/**
 * Today's capability -> the module(s) that must ALL be enabled for it.
 * `financeiro` maps only `history` (Geral settings and the archive history) here;
 * phase 3 decides what else the financeiro module gates.
 */
export const CAPABILITY_MODULES: Readonly<Record<SalesCapability, readonly CrmModuleKey[]>> = {
  proposals: ['orcamentos'],
  commissions: ['comissoes'],
  catalog: ['catalogo'],
  clients: ['clientes'],
  import: ['importacao'],
  finders: ['finders'],
  history: ['financeiro'],
  leadFullFields: ['catalogo'],
};

/**
 * The module flags that reproduce an edition exactly: a module is enabled iff every
 * capability mapped to it is granted by the edition. full => all seven;
 * leads => exactly `clientes` and `importacao`. Used by the phase 3 one-time token backfill.
 */
export function modulesForEdition(edition: SalesEdition): Record<CrmModuleKey, boolean> {
  const result = {} as Record<CrmModuleKey, boolean>;
  for (const key of CRM_MODULE_KEYS) {
    const mapped = (Object.keys(CAPABILITY_MODULES) as SalesCapability[]).filter((capability) =>
      CAPABILITY_MODULES[capability].includes(key),
    );
    result[key] = mapped.length > 0 && mapped.every((capability) => hasCapability(edition, capability));
  }
  return result;
}

export function isCrmModuleKey(value: unknown): value is CrmModuleKey {
  return typeof value === 'string' && (CRM_MODULE_KEYS as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

/** The behavior a default stage is seeded with. */
export function defaultStageBehavior(stage: Pick<DefaultPipelineStage, 'type'>): StageBehavior {
  return stage.type === 'orcamento' ? 'opens_orcamentos' : 'none';
}

/** Only a perdido exit requires a reason. */
export function defaultStageRequiresReason(stage: Pick<DefaultPipelineStage, 'exitKind'>): boolean {
  return stage.exitKind === 'perdido';
}

export interface TransitionPlanStage {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly position: number;
  readonly type: PipelineStageType;
  readonly exitKind: PipelineExitKind | null;
  readonly status: CrmStatus;
}

/**
 * The transition rule migration 0030 applies in SQL, over the ACTIVE stages of one pipeline.
 * open = type normal|orcamento ordered by (position, name); exits = type saida.
 *   open[i] -> open[i+1]; open[i+1] -> open[i]; every open -> every exit;
 *   every exit with exitKind pausado -> every open.
 * Returns unique [fromId, toId] pairs in a stable order.
 */
export function planPipelineTransitions(stages: readonly TransitionPlanStage[]): [string, string][] {
  const active = stages.filter((stage) => stage.status === 'active');
  const byOrder = (a: TransitionPlanStage, b: TransitionPlanStage) =>
    a.position - b.position || (a.name < b.name ? -1 : a.name > b.name ? 1 : 0);
  const open = active.filter((stage) => stage.type !== 'saida').sort(byOrder);
  const exits = active.filter((stage) => stage.type === 'saida').sort(byOrder);
  const pairs = new Map<string, [string, string]>();
  const add = (from: string, to: string) => pairs.set(`${from}>${to}`, [from, to]);
  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < open.length; i += 1) {
    add(open[i]!.id, open[i + 1]!.id);
    add(open[i + 1]!.id, open[i]!.id);
  }
  for (const from of open) for (const to of exits) add(from.id, to.id);
  for (const from of exits) if (from.exitKind === 'pausado') for (const to of open) add(from.id, to.id);
  return [...pairs.values()];
}

/** Key -> stage lookup of the default pipeline, for seeding and parity tests. */
export const DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGE_BY_KEY: ReadonlyMap<string, DefaultPipelineStage> = new Map(
  DEFAULT_PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stage) => [stage.key, stage]),
);

  label: 'Cliente',
  kind: 'reference',
  referenceTarget: 'cliente',
  multiple: false,
  filterable: true,
  isSystem: true,
  removable: false,
  backingColumn: 'client_id',
  position: 1,
} as const;

/**
 * The ONLY file in this slice that names a hook. Everything above it -
 * `LeadsBoard`, `LeadCard`, `MoveLeadDialog`, `LeadForm` - is pure props in,
 * callbacks out, which is what lets every board oracle drive the real components
 * with no query client at all.
 *
 * It classifies no auth or entitlement failure: `SalesOpsApp` already owns that
 * chain one level up, and a second classifier would be a second gate.
 */

const BOARD_LOAD_FAILED = 'Não foi possível carregar o funil de leads.';
const SELLER_PERSON_UNMAPPED =
  'Seu acesso ainda não está vinculado a um vendedor. Peça ao gestor para conferir seu cadastro em Vendedores.';
function sameLeadSnapshot(current: SalesOpsLead | undefined, expected: SalesOpsLead): boolean {
  // The response to the forward write is the expected server state. A fresh
  // board read must still match it before a compensating write can be offered.
  // Comparing the whole wire row also detects changes outside this edition.
  return current !== undefined && JSON.stringify(current) === JSON.stringify(expected);
}

/**
 * The board read failure copy. Only the leads edition names the one refusal an
 * operator can fix: `403 {reason:'seller_person_unmapped'}`, which survives the
 * server's auto-provision only when the cadastro is ambiguous (two pessoas, an
 * inactive one) or the token carries no e-mail. Keyed on the status plus
 * `ApiError.reason`, like every other leads-edition refusal. The full edition
 * keeps the generic copy byte-for-byte.
 */
function boardLoadErrorCopy(error: unknown, fieldSet: LeadFieldSet): string {
  if (
    fieldSet === 'contact' &&
    typeof error === 'object' &&
    error !== null &&
    (error as { status?: unknown }).status === 403 &&
    (error as { reason?: unknown }).reason === 'seller_person_unmapped'
  ) {
    return SELLER_PERSON_UNMAPPED;
  }
  return BOARD_LOAD_FAILED;
}

export type LeadsBoardContainerProps = {
  routedMode?: 'board' | 'stage';
  selectedStageId?: string;
  workspace?: SalesOpsWorkspace;
  clients: SalesOpsClient[];
  people: SalesOpsPerson[];
  products: SalesOpsProduct[];
  /** Built by the routing layer with `hasFuncao(person, FUNCAO_SLUG_VENDEDOR)`. */
  sellers: ComboboxOption[];
  /**
   * Whether to OFFER the vendedor narrowing filter. `true` under `operacional`
   * (the team board), `false` under `meus-dados`.
   *
   * It is NOT a scope switch and must never be read as one. A seller's board is
   * narrowed SERVER-side inside `withTenant` from the verified token; this boolean
   * only decides whether an admin is offered a control, and setting it `true` for
   * a seller would add a picker, not data.
   *
   * The STATE behind it lives here and not in `SalesOpsApp.tsx`, unlike
   * `productKind`: that one is hoisted because the shell's header action READS it,
   * and nothing in the shell chrome reads this one. It is component state and not
   * URL state for the same reason `productKind` is - the URL is the source of
   * truth for the painel and the page, and a narrowing filter is neither.
   */
  showSellerFilter?: boolean;
  /** Forwarded verbatim to `LeadsBoard`. The conversion flow's entire attachment surface. */
  onRequestConversion?: (request: LeadConversionRequest) => Promise<string | null>;
  /** Opens the proposta a converted lead became. Forwarded verbatim. */
  onOpenSale?: (saleId: string) => void;
  /**
   * Create a client by name inline from the LeadForm's empresa picker, in both
   * editions. Owned by `SalesOpsApp` (the one client-create seam), so this
   * container keeps naming only leads hooks. Absent means the Criar row is hidden.
   */
  onCreateClient?: (name: string) => Promise<ComboboxOption | null>;
};

export function LeadsBoardContainer({
  routedMode,
  selectedStageId,
  workspace,
  clients,
  people,
  products,
  sellers,
  showSellerFilter = false,
  onRequestConversion,
  onOpenSale,
  onCreateClient,
}: LeadsBoardContainerProps) {
  // The edition is read ONCE here and handed down as `fieldSet`, so every
  // presentational leads component stays free of auth hooks.
  const fieldSet = leadFieldSet(useSalesEdition());
  // One admin read serves both decisions: managing etapas and, in the leads
  // edition, being offered the vendedor picker (D-07.1b). A seller's own lead
  // is assigned to them by the API, so they are never asked to pick themselves.
  const isAdmin = useAuthProfile().roles.includes('admin');
  const canManageStages = isAdmin;
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [sellerPersonId, setSellerPersonId] = React.useState<string | null>(null);
  // The columns are resolved FIRST: the board query fans out one request per
  // stage and the API requires a `stageId`, so without the pipeline there is
  // nothing to ask for. The Quadro is a view of ONE pipeline: its columns are
  // that pipeline's stages, and the choice is component state (not URL state).
  const pipelinesQuery = usePipelines();
  const pipelines = React.useMemo(() => pipelinesQuery.data ?? [], [pipelinesQuery.data]);
  const [pipelineChoice, setPipelineChoice] = React.useState<string | null>(null);
  const [period, setPeriod] = React.useState<PeriodSelection>(PERIOD_ALL_SELECTION);
  const [moveError, setMoveError] = React.useState<unknown>(null);
  // A routed stage belongs to exactly one pipeline, so an `?etapa=` link lands on it.
  const owningPipelineId = selectedStageId
    ? pipelines.find((candidate) => candidate.stages.some((stage) => stage.id === selectedStageId))?.id
    : undefined;
  const pipeline = pickPipeline(pipelines, owningPipelineId ?? pipelineChoice);
  const pipelineId = pipeline?.id ?? null;
  const stages = React.useMemo(() => pipelineStagesAsBoardStages(pipeline), [pipeline]);
  const pipelineOptions = React.useMemo(
    () =>
      pipelines
        .filter((candidate) => candidate.status === 'active')
        .sort((a, b) => a.position - b.position)
        .map((candidate) => ({ value: candidate.id, label: candidate.name })),
    [pipelines],
  );
  const range = React.useMemo(() => selectionToRange(period, todayInSaoPaulo()), [period]);
  const { from: periodFrom, to: periodTo } = range;

  // A filter that is not OFFERED can never narrow: an operator who filters under
  // `operacional` and then opens their own board must not carry that narrowing
  // into a query the server has already scoped.
  const sellerFilterValue = showSellerFilter ? sellerPersonId : null;
  /*
    Memoized on the VALUE alone, and the same object reaches the board read, the
    move and the stage summary: the move patches the board and summary entries
    the board renders, so a mismatch would silently degrade to no optimistic
    write at all.
  */
  const filters = React.useMemo<LeadBoardFilters>(() => {
    const next = {
      ...(sellerFilterValue ? { sellerPersonId: sellerFilterValue } : {}),
      ...(pipelineId ? { pipelineId } : {}),
      ...periodQuery({ from: periodFrom, to: periodTo }),
    };
    return Object.keys(next).length > 0 ? next : undefined;
  }, [sellerFilterValue, pipelineId, periodFrom, periodTo]);

  const boardQuery = useLeadsBoard(stages, filters);
  const summaryQuery = useLeadStageSummary(filters, pipelinesQuery.isSuccess);
  const moveLead = useMoveLead(filters);
  const saveLead = useSaveLead();
  const deleteLead = useDeleteLead();
  const undo = useUndoNotice();

  async function requireUnchangedLead(expected: SalesOpsLead) {
    const fresh = await boardQuery.refetch({ throwOnError: true });
    if (!sameLeadSnapshot(fresh.data?.leads.find((row) => row.id === expected.id), expected)) {
      throw new UndoConflictError(UNDO_CONFLICT);
    }
  }

  async function moveWithUndo(payload: MoveLeadPayload) {
    const rows = boardQuery.data?.leads ?? [];
    const previous = rows.find((row) => row.id === payload.leadId);
    const sourceColumn = previous
      ? rows.filter((row) => row.stageId === previous.stageId).sort((a, b) => a.position - b.position)
      : [];
    const sourceIndex = sourceColumn.findIndex((row) => row.id === payload.leadId);
    const result = await moveLead.mutateAsync(payload);
    if (!previous || sourceIndex < 0 || payload.saleId ||
      stageOpensConversion(stages.find((stage) => stage.id === payload.toStageId))) return;
    undo.publish('Lead movido', async () => {
      await requireUnchangedLead(result.lead);
      return moveLead.mutateAsync({
        leadId: payload.leadId,
        toStageId: previous.stageId,
        toIndex: sourceIndex,
        ...(previous.lostReason ? { reason: previous.lostReason } : {}),
      });
    });
  }

  const lookups = React.useMemo(
    () => buildLabelLookups({ clients, people, products }),
    [clients, people, products],
  );

  /*
    Stable per mount. It used to be a bare `new Date()`, so every render handed
    every card a new object and defeated their memoisation - during a drag that
    is one full re-render of the board per pointer move. The badge it feeds
    counts DAYS, so a clock that advances only on remount is exact enough.
  */
  const now = React.useMemo(() => new Date(), []);

  const [dialogOpen, setDialogOpen] = React.useState(false);
  /** The lead whose delete confirmation is open; null when none is. */
  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = React.useState<SalesOpsLead | null>(null);

  async function confirmDelete(lead: SalesOpsLead) {
    await deleteLead.mutateAsync(lead.id);
    undo.publishRestore(lead.id);
  }

  const definitionsQuery = useFieldDefinitions();
  const definitions = definitionsQuery.data ?? [];

export type LeadFormProps = {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  /** null means create. The caller keys the form by lead id (mount-scoped state). */
  initial: SalesOpsLead | null;
  definitions: FieldDefinition[];
  clients: SalesOpsClient[];
  products: SalesOpsProduct[];
  sales?: ComboboxOption[];
  sellers: ComboboxOption[];
  showSellerPicker?: boolean;
  onCreateClient?: (name: string) => Promise<ComboboxOption | null>;
  onSubmit: (payload: LeadWritePayload) => Promise<unknown> | void;
  pending?: boolean;
  /** Create only: the pipeline the board is showing. */
  pipelineId?: string;
  onDelete?: () => void;
};

/** The ONE lead form of both editions. Logic is in lead-form.ts; this file is the view and its state. */
export function LeadForm({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  initial,
  definitions,
  clients,
  products,
  sales,
  sellers,
  showSellerPicker = true,
  onCreateClient,
  onSubmit,
  pending = false,
  pipelineId,
  onDelete,
}: LeadFormProps) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = React.useState<LeadFormDraft>(() => leadFormDraftFromLead(initial, definitions));
  const [hadValue] = React.useState<ReadonlySet<string>>(() => fieldIdsWithValue(initial));
  // A lead that never had a contact may be saved without one; the form does not force one on it.
  const [contactOptional] = React.useState(() => initial !== null && !initial.contactPhone && !initial.contactEmail);
  const [touched, setTouched] = React.useState<ReadonlySet<string>>(new Set());
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = React.useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = React.useState(false);
  const [saveError, setSaveError] = React.useState<string | null>(null);
  const [prefilled, setPrefilled] = React.useState(false);

  // The São Paulo civil day, read here (the view) and passed down; the logic modules never read the clock.
  const today = todayInSaoPaulo();
  const problem = validateLeadFormDraft(draft, definitions, { contactOptional, today });
  const showProblem = problem !== null && (submitted || touched.has(problem.field));
  const canCreateClient = Boolean(onCreateClient);
  const clientPlaceholder = clientPickerCopy(canCreateClient);
  const clientOptions = React.useMemo<ComboboxOption[]>(
    () => clients.map((row) => ({ value: row.id, label: row.name })),
    [clients],
  );

  function touch(...fields: string[]) {
    setTouched((current) => {
      const next = new Set(current);
      for (const field of fields) next.add(field);
      return next;
    });
  }

  function patch(changes: Partial<LeadFormDraft>) {
    setDraft((current) => ({ ...current, ...changes }));
  }

  function selectClient(value: string) {
    const picked = clients.find((row) => row.id === value);
    const contact = prefillContactFromClient(draft, picked);
    patch({ clientId: value === '' ? null : value, clientName: picked?.name ?? '', ...contact });
    setPrefilled(Object.keys(contact).length > 0);
  }

  async function createClient(name: string) {
    if (!onCreateClient) return;
    const created = await onCreateClient(name);
    if (!created) return;
    patch({ clientId: created.value, clientName: created.label });
  }

  function setFieldDraft(fieldId: string, next: FieldDraft) {
    touch(`field:${fieldId}`);
    setDraft((current) => ({ ...current, fields: { ...current.fields, [fieldId]: next } }));
  }

  async function save() {
    setSubmitted(true);
    if (problem !== null || submitting) return;
    setSubmitting(true);
    setSaveError(null);
    try {
      await onSubmit(
        buildLeadWritePayload(draft, definitions, {
          ...(initial?.id ? { id: initial.id } : {}),
          includeSeller: showSellerPicker,
          ...(pipelineId ? { pipelineId } : {}),
          hadValue,
          contactOptional,
        }),
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      setSaveError(leadFormSaveErrorCopy(error));
      setSubmitting(false);
      return;
    }
    setSubmitting(false);
    onOpenChange(false);
  }

  return (
    <Dialog onOpenChange={onOpenChange} open={open}>
      <DialogContent className="max-w-xl">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>{initial?.id ? LEAD_FORM_COPY.editTitle : LEAD_FORM_COPY.createTitle}</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>{LEAD_FORM_COPY.description}</DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        <div className="flex max-h-[60vh] flex-col gap-4 overflow-y-auto">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <label className={fieldLabelClass} htmlFor="lead-contact-name">
              {LEAD_FORM_COPY.nameLabel}
              <span aria-hidden="true"> *</span>
            </label>
            <Input
              className={formInputClass}
              id="lead-contact-name"
              maxLength={140}
              onChange={(event) => {
                touch('contactName');
                patch({ contactName: event.target.value });
              }}
              required
              value={draft.contactName}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                <label className={fieldLabelClass} htmlFor="lead-phone">
                  {LEAD_FORM_COPY.phoneLabel}
                </label>
                <Input
                  autoComplete="tel"
                  className={formInputClass}
                  id="lead-phone"
                  inputMode="tel"
                  maxLength={40}
                  onChange={(event) => {
                    touch('contact', 'contactEmail');
                    setPrefilled(false);
                    patch({ contactPhone: event.target.value });
                  }}
                  type="tel"
                  value={draft.contactPhone}

 exited 1 in 0ms:

import type { FinderStatus } from '@/admin/types';
import type { CommissionStatus } from '@/lib/api-client';

/**
 * The single repo-wide TanStack query-key factory. Every `useQuery` key and every
 * `useAppMutation` `invalidates` entry resolves through this object so invalidation
 * targets are declarative and impossible to typo, and so a list key can never
 * collide with a detail key (hence the explicit `'list'` / `'detail'` segments).
 *
 * `adminProducts.all` and `adminProducts.list()` are byte-identical to the keys
 * they replaced because src/admin/products/__tests__/useProducts.test.ts asserts
 * those tuples literally.
 */

type CommissionFilters = { status?: CommissionStatus; finderId?: string } | undefined;
type ConversionFilters = { source?: string; finderId?: string } | undefined;
/**
 * Declared LOCALLY, and deliberately NOT imported from sales-ops/leads/types.ts:
 * this file is imported by `admin/`, `finder/` and `lib/` and must not grow an
 * edge into a sales-ops subtree. `sales-ops/leads/types.ts` exports a
 * structurally identical alias for its own consumers, exactly as
 * `CommissionFilters` duplicates its consumers' shape here.
 */
type LeadBoardFilters =
  | { sellerPersonId?: string; pipelineId?: string; from?: string; to?: string }
  | undefined;
/** Declared locally for the same reason as `LeadBoardFilters`: the follow-ups list filters. */
type FollowupFilters = { scope: 'mine' | 'team'; status: 'open' | 'done' | 'all'; from?: string; to?: string };

export const queryKeys = {
  salesOps: {
    all: ['sales-ops'] as const,
    bootstrap: () => ['sales-ops', 'bootstrap'] as const,
    /*
      Nested under the `sales-ops` prefix on purpose: every sales-ops write already
      declares `invalidates: [queryKeys.salesOps.all]`, TanStack invalidates by
      prefix match, and so an archive performed on a list view refreshes the
      Configurações history without that mutation knowing the panel exists.
    */
    cadastroHistory: (limit: number) => ['sales-ops', 'cadastro-history', limit] as const,
    /*
      Nested under `sales-ops` for the same reason: every sales-ops write, and the
      settlement writes in particular, refresh a sale's settlement history by prefix.
    */
    saleSettlements: (saleId: string) => ['sales-ops', 'sale-settlements', saleId] as const,
  },
  adminApps: {
    all: ['admin', 'apps'] as const,
    list: () => ['admin', 'apps'] as const,
    detail: (id: string) => ['admin', 'apps', 'detail', id] as const,
  },
  adminProducts: {
    all: ['admin', 'products'] as const,
    // keep the `appId ?? 'all'` shape: useProducts.test.ts asserts it literally
    list: (appId?: string) => ['admin', 'products', appId ?? 'all'] as const,
    detail: (id: string) => ['admin', 'products', 'detail', id] as const,
  },
  adminSellers: {
    all: ['admin', 'sellers'] as const,
    list: () => ['admin', 'sellers'] as const,
  },
  adminFinders: {
    all: ['admin', 'finders'] as const,
    list: (status?: FinderStatus) => ['admin', 'finders', 'list', status ?? 'all'] as const,
    detail: (id: string) => ['admin', 'finders', 'detail', id] as const,
  },
  adminCommissions: {
    all: ['admin', 'commissions'] as const,
    list: (filters: CommissionFilters) => ['admin', 'commissions', 'list', filters ?? null] as const,
  },
  adminConversions: {
    all: ['admin', 'conversions'] as const,
    list: (filters: ConversionFilters) => ['admin', 'conversions', 'list', filters ?? null] as const,
  },
  adminAudit: {
    all: ['admin', 'audit'] as const,
    list: (page: number, action?: string) => ['admin', 'audit', 'list', page, action ?? 'all'] as const,
  },
  payouts: {
    all: ['payouts'] as const,
    findersReady: () => ['payouts', 'finders-ready'] as const,
    list: () => ['payouts', 'list'] as const,
  },
  finderLinks: {
    all: ['finder', 'links'] as const,
    list: () => ['finder', 'links'] as const,
  },
  finderCatalog: {
    all: ['finder', 'apps'] as const,
    apps: () => ['finder', 'apps'] as const,
    products: (appId?: string) => ['finder', 'apps', appId ?? 'all', 'products'] as const,
  },
  /**
   * Leads are deliberately a TOP-LEVEL root and NOT nested under `['sales-ops']`.
   *
   * Every sales-ops write declares `invalidates: [queryKeys.salesOps.all]`, and
   * TanStack invalidates by prefix match. Nesting the board there would make a
   * produto rename, an área archive and every other cadastro keystroke refetch a
   * paginated board of the highest-volume entity in the product - the exact cost
   * leads were given their own paginated endpoint to avoid.
   *
   * The separation is one-directional and intentional: a lead CONVERSION creates
   * a sale, so slice 08's conversion mutation lists BOTH `queryKeys.leads.all`
   * and `queryKeys.salesOps.all`, explicitly and type-checked, rather than
   * relying on a prefix that would also fire in the useless direction.
   *
   * Stages live under the same `['leads']` root on purpose: archiving a stage
   * removes a column, so one `invalidates: [queryKeys.leads.all]` refreshes the
   * cadastro list and the board together.
   *
   * Account- and org-agnostic, exactly like every key in this file: tenant
   * separation is `queryClient.clear()` on logout and on every completed
   * workspace switch (see apps/web/src/auth/react.tsx), never a key segment.
   */
  leads: {
    all: ['leads'] as const,
    board: (filters: LeadBoardFilters) => ['leads', 'board', filters ?? null] as const,
    /*
      The board's per-stage totals (`GET /leads/summary`). Keyed by the SAME
      filters value as `board(filters)`, so the optimistic move and delete patch
      the summary entry paired with the board entry they patch. Under the
      `['leads']` root on purpose: every lead write's `queryKeys.leads.all`
      refreshes it with no extra key on any mutation.
    */
    summary: (filters: LeadBoardFilters) => ['leads', 'summary', filters ?? null] as const,
    stages: () => ['leads', 'stages'] as const,
    deleted: () => ['leads', 'deleted'] as const,
    pipelines: () => ['leads', 'pipelines'] as const,
    fieldDefinitions: () => ['leads', 'field-definitions'] as const,
    detail: (leadId: string) => ['leads', 'detail', leadId] as const,
    followups: (filters: FollowupFilters) => ['leads', 'followups', filters] as const,
  },
  finderClicks: {
    all: ['finder', 'clicks'] as const,
    list: (linkId?: string) => ['finder', 'clicks', 'list', linkId ?? 'all'] as const,
    stats: () => ['finder', 'clicks', 'stats'] as const,
  },
} as const;

describe('notes and follow-ups', () => {
  it('POST notes and followups answer 201 with the created row; blank input is 400', async () => {
    const w = await world('notes');
    const lead = await w.create();
    const note = await call(w.app, 'POST', `/leads/${lead.id}/notes`, { body: '  Ligou  ' });
    expect(note.status).toBe(201);
    expect(note.json.note).toMatchObject({ body: 'Ligou', authorName: 'Gestora Ana' });
    expect((await call(w.app, 'POST', `/leads/${lead.id}/notes`, { body: '  ' })).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await call(w.app, 'POST', `/leads/${randomUUID()}/notes`, { body: 'x' })).status).toBe(404);
    const followup = await call(w.app, 'POST', `/leads/${lead.id}/followups`, { nextStep: 'Enviar proposta', dueOn: '2026-10-22' });
    expect(followup.status).toBe(201);
    expect(followup.json.followup).toMatchObject({ nextStep: 'Enviar proposta', dueOn: '2026-10-22', doneAt: null });
    expect((await call(w.app, 'POST', `/leads/${lead.id}/followups`, { nextStep: 'x' })).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await call(w.app, 'POST', `/leads/${lead.id}/followups`, { nextStep: 'x', dueOn: '2026-10-22', ownerPersonId: randomUUID() })).json).toMatchObject({ reason: 'owner_not_found' });
  });

  it('GET /followups: mine vs team, status and a due-day period; team needs an admin; done is idempotent', async () => {
    const w = await world('list');
    const db = getDb();
    const ana = await createPerson(db, w.orgId, PersonSchema.parse({ displayName: 'Ana Vendedora', isSeller: true, contactEmail: 'ana@x.test' }));
    if (typeof ana === 'string') throw new Error(ana);
    await sql`UPDATE sales_ops_people SET hub_account_id = 'hub_ana' WHERE id = ${ana.id}`;
    const seller: Who = { userId: 'hub_ana', role: 'seller', name: 'Ana Vendedora' };
    const bruno = await createPerson(db, w.orgId, PersonSchema.parse({ displayName: 'Bruno', isSeller: true }));
    if (typeof bruno === 'string') throw new Error(bruno);
    await sql`UPDATE sales_ops_people SET hub_account_id = 'hub_bruno' WHERE id = ${bruno.id}`;
    const brunoWho: Who = { userId: 'hub_bruno', role: 'seller', name: 'Bruno' };

    const anaLead = await w.create({ contactName: 'Lead da Ana', sellerPersonId: ana.id });
    const brunoLead = await w.create({ contactName: 'Lead do Bruno', sellerPersonId: bruno.id });
    const a1 = await call(appFor(w.orgId, seller), 'POST', `/leads/${anaLead.id}/followups`, { nextStep: 'A1', dueOn: '2026-10-20' });
    await call(appFor(w.orgId, seller), 'POST', `/leads/${anaLead.id}/followups`, { nextStep: 'A2', dueOn: '2026-11-05' });
    await call(appFor(w.orgId, brunoWho), 'POST', `/leads/${brunoLead.id}/followups`, { nextStep: 'B1', dueOn: '2026-10-21' });

    const mine = await call(appFor(w.orgId, seller), 'GET', '/followups?scope=mine');
    expect(mine.json.followups.map((f: { nextStep: string }) => f.nextStep)).toEqual(['A1', 'A2']);
    expect(mine.json.followups[0]).toEqual({ id: expect.any(String), leadId: anaLead.id, leadContactName: 'Lead da Ana', nextStep: 'A1', dueOn: '2026-10-20', ownerName: 'Ana Vendedora', doneAt: null });
    expect((await call(appFor(w.orgId, seller), 'GET', '/followups?scope=team')).status).toBe(403);
    const team = await call(w.app, 'GET', '/followups?scope=team');
    expect(team.json.followups.map((f: { nextStep: string }) => f.nextStep)).toEqual(['A1', 'B1', 'A2']);
    const period = await call(w.app, 'GET', '/followups?scope=team&from=2026-10-21&to=2026-10-31');
    expect(period.json.followups.map((f: { nextStep: string }) => f.nextStep)).toEqual(['B1']);
    expect((await call(w.app, 'GET', '/followups?from=2026-10-31&to=2026-10-01')).status).toBe(400);

    // A colleague cannot see or complete it; the owner can, twice, keeping the first done_at.
    expect((await call(appFor(w.orgId, brunoWho), 'POST', `/followups/${a1.json.followup.id}/done`)).status).toBe(404);
    const done = await call(appFor(w.orgId, seller), 'POST', `/followups/${a1.json.followup.id}/done`);
    expect(done.status).toBe(200);
    const again = await call(appFor(w.orgId, seller), 'POST', `/followups/${a1.json.followup.id}/done`);
    expect(again.json.followup.doneAt).toBe(done.json.followup.doneAt);
    const open = await call(appFor(w.orgId, seller), 'GET', '/followups?scope=mine&status=open');
    expect(open.json.followups.map((f: { nextStep: string }) => f.nextStep)).toEqual(['A2']);
    const doneList = await call(appFor(w.orgId, seller), 'GET', '/followups?scope=mine&status=done');
    expect(doneList.json.followups.map((f: { nextStep: string }) => f.nextStep)).toEqual(['A1']);
  });
});

   389	/** Idempotent: marking an already-done follow-up keeps its original `done_at`. */
   390	export function markFollowupDone(db: Db, orgId: string, followupId: string, scope: LeadScope): Promise<DoneResult> {
   391	  return withTenant(db, orgId, async (tx) => {
   392	    const gate = await resolveLeadScopePredicate(tx, orgId, scope);
   393	    if (!gate.ok) return { ok: false, reason: gate.reason } as const;
   394	    const [row] = await tx
   395	      .select({ followup: salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups })
   396	      .from(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups)
   397	      .innerJoin(
   398	        salesOpsCrmLeads,
   399	        and(eq(salesOpsCrmLeads.orgId, salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.orgId), eq(salesOpsCrmLeads.id, salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.leadId)),
   400	      )
   401	      .where(
   402	        and(
   403	          eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.orgId, orgId),
   404	          eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.id, followupId),
   405	          liveLeadCondition(),
   406	          ...(gate.sellerPersonId ? [leadSellerCondition(gate.sellerPersonId, gate.canClaimUnassigned)] : []),
   407	        ),
   408	      )
   409	      .limit(1)
   410	      .for('update', { of: salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups });
   411	    if (!row) return { ok: false, reason: 'not_found' } as const;
   412	    if (row.followup.doneAt !== null) return { ok: true, followup: toFollowupView(row.followup) } as const;
   413	    const [done] = await tx
   414	      .update(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups)
   415	      .set({ doneAt: new Date(), updatedAt: new Date() })
   416	      .where(and(eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.orgId, orgId), eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.id, followupId)))
   417	      .returning();
   418	    return { ok: true, followup: toFollowupView(done!) } as const;
   419	  });
   420	}
   421	
   422	export type FollowupListRow = {
   423	  id: string;
   424	  leadId: string;
   425	  leadContactName: string;
   426	  nextStep: string;
   427	  dueOn: string;
   428	  ownerName: string | null;
   429	  doneAt: string | null;
   430	};
   431	
   432	export type ListFollowupsResult =
   433	  | { ok: true; followups: FollowupListRow[] }
   434	  | { ok: false; reason: 'seller_person_unmapped' };
   435	
   436	/**
   437	 * `mine`: the caller's pessoa owns it, or the caller created it. `team`: every follow-up of the org
   438	 * (the route admits only an admin). The period is on `due_on`, a civil `date`, so the days are
   439	 * compared as written and no timezone is involved. Deleted leads' follow-ups are not listed.
   440	 */
   441	export function listFollowups(db: Db, orgId: string, query: ListFollowupsQuery, scope: LeadScope): Promise<ListFollowupsResult> {
   442	  return withTenant(db, orgId, async (tx) => {
   443	    const conditions: SQL[] = [eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.orgId, orgId), liveLeadCondition()];
   444	    if (query.scope === 'mine') {
   445	      const personId = await resolveCallerPersonId(tx, orgId, scope);
   446	      const mine: SQL[] = [eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.createdByUserId, scope.userId)];
   447	      if (personId) mine.push(eq(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.ownerPersonId, personId));
   448	      conditions.push(mine.length === 1 ? mine[0]! : sql`(${mine[0]} OR ${mine[1]})`);
   449	    }
   450	    if (query.status === 'open') conditions.push(isNull(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.doneAt));
   451	    if (query.status === 'done') conditions.push(sql`${salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.doneAt} IS NOT NULL`);
   452	    if (query.from) conditions.push(sql`${salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.dueOn} >= ${query.from}::date`);
   453	    if (query.to) conditions.push(sql`${salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.dueOn} <= ${query.to}::date`);
   454	    const rows = await tx
   455	      .select({ followup: salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups, contactName: salesOpsCrmLeads.contactName })
   456	      .from(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups)
   457	      .innerJoin(
   458	        salesOpsCrmLeads,
   459	        and(eq(salesOpsCrmLeads.orgId, salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.orgId), eq(salesOpsCrmLeads.id, salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.leadId)),
   460	      )
   461	      .where(and(...conditions))
   462	      .orderBy(asc(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.dueOn), asc(salesOpsCrmLeadFollowups.id))
   463	      .limit(query.limit);
   464	    return {
   465	      ok: true,
   466	      followups: rows.map(({ followup, contactName }) => ({
   467	        id: followup.id,
   468	        leadId: followup.leadId,
   469	        leadContactName: contactName,
   470	        nextStep: followup.nextStep,
   471	        dueOn: followup.dueOn,
   472	        ownerName: followup.ownerNameSnapshot,
   473	        doneAt: iso(followup.doneAt),
   474	      })),
   475	    } as const;
   476	  });
   477	}

nexo/plans/vendas-crm-unificado/07a-api-crm-cutover.md:121:The transition statement of the repeated 0030 body is guarded by `NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sales_ops_pipeline_transitions t WHERE t.pipeline_id = s.pipeline_id)`.    190	async function addTransitionsForNewStage(
   191	  tx: Db,
   192	  orgId: string,
   193	  pipelineId: string,
   194	  stageId: string,
   195	): Promise<void> {
   196	  const stages = await listPipelineStages(tx, orgId, pipelineId);
   197	  const plan = stages.map((stage) => ({ ...stage, type: stage.type as TransitionPlanStage['type'], exitKind: stage.exitKind as TransitionPlanStage['exitKind'], status: stage.status as TransitionPlanStage['status'] }));
   198	  const pairs = planPipelineTransitions(plan).filter(([from, to]) => from === stageId || to === stageId);
   199	  if (pairs.length === 0) return;
   200	  await tx
   201	    .insert(salesOpsPipelineTransitions)
   202	    .values(pairs.map(([fromStageId, toStageId]) => ({ orgId, pipelineId, fromStageId, toStageId })))
   203	    .onConflictDoNothing();
   204	}

   206	export function updateLeadStage(
   207	  db: Db,
   208	  orgId: string,
   209	  id: string,
   210	  data: Partial<LeadStageInput>,
   211	): Promise<LeadStageRow | null | 'is_system' | LeadStageDuplicate> {
   212	  return withTenant(db, orgId, async (tx) => {
   213	    const [current] = await tx
   214	      .select()
   215	      .from(salesOpsPipelineStages)
   216	      .where(and(eq(salesOpsPipelineStages.orgId, orgId), eq(salesOpsPipelineStages.id, id)))
   217	      .limit(1)
   218	      .for('update');
   219	    if (!current) return null;
   220	    // Byte-for-byte the funções rule: a system stage (the conversion and the lost etapa) is fully
   221	    // immutable through this endpoint - no rename, no archive, no restore. Its POSITION is still
   222	    // reorderable, which reorderLeadStages does without going through here.
   223	    if (legacyStageIsSystem(current) && (data.name !== undefined || data.status !== undefined)) {
   224	      return 'is_system' as const;

   228	      // Fast path for the error message only; the unique index is the real guard.
   229	      const clash = await findLeadStageClash(tx, orgId, current.pipelineId, data.name, id);
   230	      if (clash) return clash;
   231	    }
   232	
   233	    // UPDATE has no ON CONFLICT clause, so the same TOCTOU race as
   234	    // createLeadStage is handled by catching the unique violation. The write runs
   235	    // inside a SAVEPOINT (a nested drizzle transaction) so a rejected rename
   236	    // leaves the surrounding transaction usable rather than poisoned.
   237	    let stage: LeadStageRow | null;
   238	    try {
   239	      stage = await tx.transaction(async (nested) => {
   240	        const [row] = await nested
   241	          .update(salesOpsPipelineStages)
   242	          .set({
   243	            ...data,
   244	            ...archivedAtPatch(current.status, data.status ?? current.status),
   245	            updatedAt: new Date(),
   246	          })
   247	          .where(and(eq(salesOpsPipelineStages.orgId, orgId), eq(salesOpsPipelineStages.id, id)))
   248	          .returning();
   249	        return row ? toLeadStageView(row) : null;
   250	      });
   251	    } catch (error) {
   252	      const violated = mapLeadStageUniqueViolation(error);
   253	      if (violated) return violated;
   254	      throw error;
   255	    }
   256	    // Deliberately NO auditCadastroLifecycle call. The ledger is a closed wire
   257	    // contract whose CadastroEntityTypeSchema is the four audited cadastros, and
   258	    // a fifth entity type would be an entity the history UI cannot render and the
   259	    // nightly purge would eventually hard-delete a stage leads still point at.
   260	    return stage;

+import { and, asc, eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
+import type { getDb } from '../../db/client.js';
+import { salesOpsPipelines, salesOpsPipelineStages } from '../../db/schema.js';
+import type { PipelineStageRow } from './stage-view.js';
+
+/**
+ * Read helpers for the unified pipeline tables. Every function takes the tenant transaction its
+ * caller already opened and filters by `org_id` itself (defence in depth: over the `app.fxl_admin`
+ * connection RLS shows every org, and this filter is then the only scoping).
+ */
+type Db = ReturnType<typeof getDb>;
+
+export type PipelineRow = typeof salesOpsPipelines.$inferSelect;
+
+/**
+ * The org's default pipeline id: the `is_default` row, else the first active pipeline by board
+ * order. Null only when the org has no pipeline at all (callers that must write seed first).
+ */
+export async function defaultPipelineId(tx: Db, orgId: string): Promise<string | null> {
+  const [row] = await tx
+    .select({ id: salesOpsPipelines.id })
+    .from(salesOpsPipelines)
+    .where(and(eq(salesOpsPipelines.orgId, orgId), eq(salesOpsPipelines.status, 'active')))
+    .orderBy(asc(salesOpsPipelines.isDefault), asc(salesOpsPipelines.position), asc(salesOpsPipelines.name))
+    .limit(1);
+  return row?.id ?? null;
+}
+
+export async function pipelineExists(tx: Db, orgId: string, pipelineId: string): Promise<boolean> {
+  const [row] = await tx
+    .select({ id: salesOpsPipelines.id })
+    .from(salesOpsPipelines)
+    .where(and(eq(salesOpsPipelines.orgId, orgId), eq(salesOpsPipelines.id, pipelineId)))
+    .limit(1);
+  return row !== undefined;
+}
+
+/** A pipeline's stages in board order, archived ones included. */
+export function listPipelineStages(tx: Db, orgId: string, pipelineId: string): Promise<PipelineStageRow[]> {
+  return tx
+    .select()
+    .from(salesOpsPipelineStages)
+    .where(and(eq(salesOpsPipelineStages.orgId, orgId), eq(salesOpsPipelineStages.pipelineId, pipelineId)))
+    .orderBy(asc(salesOpsPipelineStages.position), asc(salesOpsPipelineStages.name));
+}

+  birthDateFuture: 'A data de aniversário não pode ser no futuro.',
+  noStageError: 'Nenhuma etapa ativa no funil. O lead não foi salvo.',
+  saveFailed: 'Não foi possível salvar o lead. Tente novamente.',
+} as const;
+
+export type FieldDraft = string | FieldReferenceItem[];
+export type LeadFormDraft = {
+  contactName: string;
+  contactPhone: string;
+  contactEmail: string;
+  clientId: string | null;
+  clientName: string;
+  estimatedValue: string;
+  sellerPersonId: string | null;
+  fields: Record<string, FieldDraft>;
+};
+/** `field` is `contactName`, `contact`, `contactEmail` or `field:<fieldId>`. */
+export type LeadFormProblem = { field: string; message: string };
+
+export function isClienteDefinition(definition: FieldDefinition): boolean {
+  return definition.referenceTarget === 'cliente';
+}
+
+/** The dynamic fields the form renders: every definition except the Cliente, in position order. */
+export function renderableDefinitions(definitions: readonly FieldDefinition[]): FieldDefinition[] {
+  return definitions.filter((d) => !isClienteDefinition(d)).sort((a, b) => a.position - b.position);
+}
+
+const SEED_BY_KIND: Record<FieldKind, (value: LeadFieldValue['value'] | undefined) => FieldDraft> = {
+  text: (v) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v : ''),
+  number: (v) => (typeof v === 'number' ? String(v) : typeof v === 'string' ? v : ''),
+  money: (v) => (typeof v === 'number' ? (v / 100).toFixed(2) : ''),
+  date: (v) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v : ''),
+  options: (v) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v : ''),
+  reference: (v) => (Array.isArray(v) ? v.map((item) => ({ id: item.id, name: item.name })) : []),
+};
+
+export function leadFormDraftFromLead(lead: SalesOpsLead | null, definitions: readonly FieldDefinition[]): LeadFormDraft {
+  const fields: Record<string, FieldDraft> = {};
+  for (const definition of renderableDefinitions(definitions)) {
+    const stored = lead?.fields?.find((f) => f.fieldId === definition.id)?.value;
+    fields[definition.id] = SEED_BY_KIND[definition.kind](stored);
+  }
+  return {
+    contactName: lead?.contactName ?? '',
+    contactPhone: lead?.contactPhone ?? '',
+    contactEmail: lead?.contactEmail ?? '',
+    clientId: lead?.clientId ?? null,
+    clientName: lead?.clientNameSnapshot ?? '',
+    estimatedValue: lead?.estimatedValueBrl ? (lead.estimatedValueBrl / 100).toFixed(2) : '',
+    sellerPersonId: lead?.sellerPersonId ?? null,
+    fields,
+  };
+}
+
+function hasStoredValue(value: LeadFieldValue['value'] | undefined): boolean {
+  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') return false;
+  return !(Array.isArray(value) && value.length === 0);
+}
+
+/** Ids of the dynamic fields that hold a value today; the Cliente is excluded (it is not a field entry). */
+export function fieldIdsWithValue(lead: SalesOpsLead | null): Set<string> {
+  const ids = new Set<string>();
+  for (const field of lead?.fields ?? []) {
+    if (field.key !== CLIENTE_FIELD_KEY && hasStoredValue(field.value)) ids.add(field.fieldId);
+  }
+  return ids;
+}
+
+export function splitClientContact(contact: string | null | undefined): { phone: string; email: string } {
+  const trimmed = (contact ?? '').trim();
+  if (trimmed === '') return { phone: '', email: '' };
+  if (trimmed.includes('@')) return { phone: '', email: trimmed };
+  return { phone: trimmed, email: '' };
+}
+
+/**
+ * Prefills only the contact. A cliente has no responsible seller (no column), so the
+ * vendedor is never touched. Typed text is never overwritten.
+ */
+export function prefillContactFromClient(
+  draft: Pick<LeadFormDraft, 'contactPhone' | 'contactEmail'>,
+  client: { contact: string | null } | undefined,
+): Partial<Pick<LeadFormDraft, 'contactPhone' | 'contactEmail'>> {
+  if (!client) return {};
+  const split = splitClientContact(client.contact);
+  const patch: Partial<Pick<LeadFormDraft, 'contactPhone' | 'contactEmail'>> = {};
+  if (draft.contactPhone.trim() === '' && split.phone !== '') patch.contactPhone = split.phone;
+  if (draft.contactEmail.trim() === '' && split.email !== '') patch.contactEmail = split.email;
+  return patch;
+}
+
+const EMAIL_PATTERN = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
+
+const FIELD_CHECK_BY_KIND: Record<FieldKind, (draft: FieldDraft | undefined) => string | null> = {
+  number: (draft) => checkNumber(draft),
+  money: (draft) => checkNumber(draft),
+  date: (draft) => (typeof draft === 'string' && draft.trim() !== '' && !isIsoDay(draft.trim()) ? LEAD_FORM_COPY.dateInvalid : null),
+  text: () => null,
+  options: () => null,
+  reference: () => null,
+};
+
+export function isBirthDateDefinition(definition: FieldDefinition): boolean {
+  return definition.kind === 'date' && definition.key === BIRTH_DATE_FIELD_KEY;
+}
+
+/** A valid ISO day strictly after `today`. Both sides are civil days compared as strings. */
+function isFutureIsoDay(draft: FieldDraft | undefined, today: string): boolean {
+  if (typeof draft !== 'string') return false;
+  const day = draft.trim();
+  return isIsoDay(day) && day > today;
+}
+
+function checkNumber(draft: FieldDraft | undefined): string | null {
+  if (typeof draft !== 'string' || draft.trim() === '') return null;
+  return Number.isFinite(Number(draft)) ? null : LEAD_FORM_COPY.numberInvalid;
+}
+
+/**
+ * `contactOptional` is set by the caller for an EDIT of a lead that never had a contact
+ * (most legacy leads). The API accepts a row without contact, so the form does not force one
+ * on it; a new lead, or one that already has a contact, still needs one.
+ * `today` is the São Paulo civil day the caller passes (`todayInSaoPaulo()`); this module never reads the clock.
+ */
+export function validateLeadFormDraft(
+  draft: LeadFormDraft,
+  definitions: readonly FieldDefinition[],
+  options: { contactOptional?: boolean; today: string },
+): LeadFormProblem | null {
+  if (draft.contactName.trim() === '') return { field: 'contactName', message: LEAD_FORM_COPY.nameRequired };
+  if (!options.contactOptional && draft.contactPhone.trim() === '' && draft.contactEmail.trim() === '') {
+    return { field: 'contact', message: LEAD_FORM_COPY.contactRequired };
+  }
+  if (draft.contactEmail.trim() !== '' && !EMAIL_PATTERN.test(draft.contactEmail.trim())) {
+    return { field: 'contactEmail', message: LEAD_FORM_COPY.emailInvalid };
+  }
+  for (const definition of renderableDefinitions(definitions)) {
+    const message = FIELD_CHECK_BY_KIND[definition.kind](draft.fields[definition.id]);
+    if (message !== null) return { field: `field:${definition.id}`, message };
+    if (isBirthDateDefinition(definition) && isFutureIsoDay(draft.fields[definition.id], options.today)) {
+      return { field: `field:${definition.id}`, message: LEAD_FORM_COPY.birthDateFuture };
+    }
+  }
+  return null;
+}
+
+function trimmedOrNull(value: string): string | null {
+  const trimmed = value.trim();
+  return trimmed === '' ? null : trimmed;
+}
+
+const WRITE_BY_KIND: Record<FieldKind, (draft: FieldDraft | undefined) => LeadFieldWriteValue> = {
+  text: (draft) => (typeof draft === 'string' && draft.trim() !== '' ? draft.trim() : null),
+  options: (draft) => (typeof draft === 'string' && draft.trim() !== '' ? draft.trim() : null),
+  date: (draft) => (typeof draft === 'string' && draft.trim() !== '' ? draft.trim() : null),
+  number: (draft) => (typeof draft === 'string' && draft.trim() !== '' ? Number(draft) : null),
+  money: (draft) => (typeof draft === 'string' && draft.trim() !== '' ? parseCurrencyInputToCents(draft) : null),
+  reference: (draft) => {
+    if (!Array.isArray(draft)) return null;
+    const items = draft.map((item) => ({ id: item.id, name: item.name.trim() })).filter((item) => item.name !== '');
+    return items.length > 0 ? items : null;
+  },
+};
+
+export function fieldDraftToWrite(definition: FieldDefinition, draft: FieldDraft | undefined): LeadFieldWriteValue {
+  return WRITE_BY_KIND[definition.kind](draft);
+}
+
+export function buildLeadWritePayload(
+  draft: LeadFormDraft,
+  definitions: readonly FieldDefinition[],
+  options: {
+    id?: string;
+    includeSeller: boolean;
+    pipelineId?: string;
+    hadValue?: ReadonlySet<string>;
+    /** Edit of a contact-less lead that still has none: the contact keys are omitted, not sent as null. */
+    contactOptional?: boolean;
+  },
+): LeadWritePayload {
+  const phone = trimmedOrNull(draft.contactPhone);
+  const email = trimmedOrNull(draft.contactEmail);
+  const omitContact = options.contactOptional === true && phone === null && email === null;
+  const fields: LeadFieldWrite[] = [];
+  for (const definition of renderableDefinitions(definitions)) {
+    const value = fieldDraftToWrite(definition, draft.fields[definition.id]);
+    if (value !== null || options.hadValue?.has(definition.id)) fields.push({ fieldId: definition.id, value });
+  }
+  return {
+    ...(options.id ? { id: options.id } : {}),
+    contactName: draft.contactName.trim(),
+    ...(omitContact ? {} : { contactPhone: phone, contactEmail: email }),
+    clientId: draft.clientId,
+    clientName: trimmedOrNull(draft.clientName),
+    estimatedValueBrl: parseCurrencyInputToCents(draft.estimatedValue),
+    ...(options.includeSeller ? { sellerPersonId: draft.sellerPersonId } : {}),
+    ...(!options.id && options.pipelineId ? { pipelineId: options.pipelineId } : {}),
+    fields,
+  };
+}
+
+/**
+ * The full previous state as a write body, used by the edit undo. The Cliente is
+ * carried ONLY by `clientId`/`clientName`: the API refuses `clientId` together with a
+ * Cliente entry of `fields` (400 field_invalid), so that entry is dropped here.
+ */
+export function leadToWriteSeed(lead: SalesOpsLead, clearFieldIds: readonly string[] = []): LeadWritePayload {
+  const kept: LeadFieldWrite[] = (lead.fields ?? [])
+    .filter((f) => f.key !== CLIENTE_FIELD_KEY)
+    .map((f) => ({ fieldId: f.fieldId, value: f.value }));
+  return {
+    id: lead.id,
+    contactName: lead.contactName,
+    contactPhone: lead.contactPhone,
+    contactEmail: lead.contactEmail,
+    clientId: lead.clientId,
+    clientName: lead.clientNameSnapshot || null,
+    estimatedValueBrl: lead.estimatedValueBrl,
+    sellerPersonId: lead.sellerPersonId,
+    fields: [...kept, ...clearFieldIds.map((fieldId) => ({ fieldId, value: null }))],
+  };
+}
+
+export function leadFormSaveErrorCopy(error: unknown): string {
+  if (
+    typeof error === 'object' &&
+    error !== null &&
+    (error as { status?: unknown }).status === 400 &&
+    (error as { reason?: unknown }).reason === 'no_open_stage'
+  ) {
+    return LEAD_FORM_COPY.noStageError;
+  }
+  return LEAD_FORM_COPY.saveFailed;
+}
